## Production incident: tesseract.js crashed every image upload (this round)

**What happened**: the user reported Vercel **Production** logs showing `recognizePriceFromPhoto` (the free OCR fallback added in prior rounds) crashing the entire Node process on every image message: `Error: Cannot find module '/ROOT/node_modules/tesseract.js/src/worker-script/node/index.js'`, `Node.js process exited with exit status: 129`. This wasn't a graceful OCR failure — it took down all of `handleImageMessage`, so the customer's photo never got written to the DB at all, on **every single image message**, in Production.

- **This was the first indication Production is live and receiving real traffic for this feature.** Nothing in this file's prior rounds ever recorded deploying this feature (or anything) to Production — seeing this crash in a real Production log, and then confirming via `vercel ls --prod`/`vercel alias ls` that Production deploys to `pinkkkuin-shop.vercel.app` happen independently and frequently (see baseline note above), was the first evidence of that. How/when the LINE webhook got pointed at Production, the 6 migrations got applied there, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` etc. got configured — none of that is known or documented anywhere in this file. Worth asking the user directly next time Production state matters, rather than assuming the "Production go-live checklist" section further below (written when this feature only existed on Staging) still reflects reality.
- **Step 1 — immediate hotfix (deployed to Production within minutes of the report)**: removed the `recognizePriceFromPhoto` call from `handleImageMessage` entirely (not wrapped in try/catch — the crash kills the whole process before our own code's try/catch would ever run, so wrapping it would not have helped). Restores exactly the pre-OCR-feature behavior: `unit_price` stays `null` when Anthropic doesn't find one, same as before this OCR feature ever existed — not a regression, a return to the last known-good state. Verified locally (`tsc`/`eslint`/`npm run build`), then deployed with `vercel deploy --prod --yes` **and explicitly re-aliased to `pinkkkuin-shop.vercel.app`** (the first `--prod` deploy only auto-aliases to the generic `pinkkkuin-shop-lilys-projects-2a8e834c.vercel.app` URL — the actual custom domain alias has to be set separately with `vercel alias set`, or the fix silently doesn't reach real traffic). Confirmed via `curl -X POST https://pinkkkuin-shop.vercel.app/api/line/webhook` returning `401` (not `500`) that Production was no longer crashing.
- **Step 2 — root-caused and fixed properly, verified on a real Vercel Staging deployment (not just local checks) before being trusted**:
  - `outputFileTracingIncludes` (the standard, commonly-cited fix for "file missing after Vercel deploy") was tried first and **did not work** — confirmed by inspecting the actual `.next/server/app/api/line/webhook/route.js.nft.json` trace file locally (it *did* list `worker-script/node/index.js` and every dependency), yet the identical crash still happened on a real Staging deploy with that fix in place. This ruled out "file not included in the deployment" as the actual cause.
  - Real root cause (confirmed via [naptha/tesseract.js#868](https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/issues/868), a well-documented Next.js/Vercel-specific issue): tesseract.js spawns its OCR worker via `new Worker(path.join(__dirname, ...))` (`src/worker/node/spawnWorker.js`). Turbopack rewrites `__dirname` for bundled modules so it no longer matches where the file actually lives after deployment — the worker thread's own independent module resolution then looks in the wrong place, regardless of whether the right file bytes were included somewhere in the deployment.
  - **The actual fix, matching the two independently-reported working fixes on that GitHub issue**: (1) added `"tesseract.js"` and `"tesseract.js-core"` to `serverExternalPackages` in `next.config.ts` (alongside `"sharp"`, same mechanism as the earlier sharp fix) — this stops Turbopack from touching tesseract.js's own source files at all, so its real on-disk `__dirname` stays correct; (2) passed an explicit `workerPath: "./node_modules/tesseract.js/src/worker-script/node/index.js"` option to `createWorker()` in `price-ocr.ts`, instead of relying on tesseract.js's own computed default — this is resolved relative to the function's working directory at runtime, not a bundler-mangled `__dirname`. `outputFileTracingIncludes` was kept as well (still needed to get the actual `.wasm` core files included in the deployment at all — just not sufficient by itself).
  - **Verified against a real Vercel Staging deployment, not just local tooling** (the explicit lesson from this incident — local `tsc`/`eslint`/`npm run build`/direct `node` runs had all passed throughout, including on the version that crashed in Production): added a temporary diagnostic route (`/api/line/debug-ocr-test`, gated behind the existing backend admin session auth, since the normal path to this code — a real bound LINE test account sending a photo — is blocked by the same long-standing pending-migrations issue that has blocked all end-to-end testing of this entire feature). Logged into the Staging backend via `curl` to get a session cookie, then POSTed each of the 4 real reference photos (`scratch/price-ocr-samples/`) directly to that route against the **actual deployed Staging function**. Result: `280`/`270`/`280`/`null` — exactly matching the expected local results from the prior round, no crash. Cross-checked with `vercel logs` across all test calls — zero `error`-level entries, no "Cannot find module", no exit-129 signatures. Deleted the diagnostic route immediately afterward (its job was done, and it's not something that should linger even behind auth).
- **Current state**: the real fix (commit `32f8a08`) is deployed and verified on **Staging only**. `recognizePriceFromPhoto`'s call site in `handleImageMessage` is back in place in the `official-next` source. **Production still has the Step 1 hotfix (OCR fallback removed entirely)** — the real fix has deliberately not been promoted to Production yet, since that wasn't asked for in this round (scoped explicitly to "restore Production immediately, fix properly on Staging, no rush on re-promoting"). Promoting the real fix to Production is a decision for the user to make explicitly, the same way the emergency hotfix itself required an explicit "deploy immediately" instruction before any Production action was taken this round.

## LINE 圖片下單 + 社群連線訂單 (new feature, this round — not yet usable end-to-end)

A brand-new, fully independent feature: customers order by sending photos of products directly to the LINE Official Account chat, no website involved. Does **not** touch `community_orders`/`community_order_items`/the existing 記事本 system in any way, and does **not** touch the existing admin-approval binding review page/API (`社群名單` tab, `/api/backend/community/members`) at all. The feature originally wrote into `community_line_bindings` for its own customer-facing nickname-approval flow, but that whole mechanism (暱稱綁定/審核) was **removed** in a later round — see 功能一 below — so this feature no longer reads or writes `community_line_bindings` in any way; nickname is now just the customer's live LINE display name.

**Entirely reply-based, zero push, by deliberate design.** The feature originally had one push (訂單已確認, on 購買狀態→已購買) — it was removed in a later round because the user decided it wasn't needed; every remaining message anywhere in this feature (image upload, self-service remittance, self-service cancel) already only ever used `sendLineReply`/`sendLineReplyText`/`sendLineReplyFlex`, which don't count against the LINE OA's monthly message quota. Backend edits (including marking a row 已購買) are now pure database updates with no customer-facing side effect at all — see 功能四 below for the removal detail.

### Configurable trigger keywords (cuts across 功能一/二/五/六)

Six trigger-keyword groups that used to be (or would otherwise have been) hardcoded arrays in `src/lib/line/livestream-orders.ts` are backend-editable, **no new migration**:

- `src/lib/line/livestream-keywords.ts` — `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_KEYWORDS` holds the default list for all 6 groups (`order`/`remittance`/`cancel`/`order_query`/`photo_confirm`/`remittance_cancel`). `getLivestreamKeywordSettings()` (the read entry point, **reshaped this round** — see the per-group enable/disable bullet below) reads the admin-saved override from `schedule_settings` (`type='community-livestream-keywords'`, JSON in `image` — same generic-KV pattern as `livestream-bank-info.ts`/`community-notification-templates.ts`, no new table) and **falls back to the defaults on any read failure or if nothing's been saved yet** — so a backend that's never touched this settings screen behaves exactly as before. `handleTextMessage` calls this once per incoming text message (a single extra query, not one per keyword-group check) rather than reading module-level constants. The underlying `sanitizeAllGroups` helper (keywords) and a parallel `sanitizeEnabled` helper both loop over `LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUPS` instead of naming each group by hand — so a further new group only needs to be added to the `LivestreamKeywordGroup` type, `LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUPS`, `LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUP_LABELS`, `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_KEYWORDS`, and `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_ENABLED`.
- **Per-group enable/disable switch (this round)**: the `schedule_settings` JSON shape changed from a bare `Record<group, string[]>` to `{ groups: Record<group, string[]>, enabled: Record<group, boolean> }`. **Backward-compatible on read**: `parseStoredSettings` checks whether the parsed JSON has a `groups` or `enabled` key at all — if neither is present, the whole parsed value *is* the old bare `Record<group, string[]>`, which gets treated as `groups` with every group's `enabled` defaulting to `true` (verified with a throwaway `node -e` simulation of both the old-shape and new-shape JSON before trusting it — old data round-trips to "all 6 enabled," new data's explicit `false` values are honored). `getLivestreamKeywords()` (the old, enabled-blind read function) was deleted outright rather than kept as a compatibility shim — grepped first and confirmed its only two callers (this route's `GET` and `handleTextMessage`) both needed the `enabled` data anyway and were switched to call `getLivestreamKeywordSettings()` directly. `saveLivestreamKeywords(keywords, enabled)` now takes and persists both. In `handleTextMessage`, every single `matchesAny(text, keywords.X)` call site was replaced with a new `matchesEnabledGroup(text, keywords, enabled, "X")` helper that checks `enabled[group]` **first** and short-circuits to `false` (not "skip the check" — an explicit non-match) if that group is disabled — this covers all three call sites that existed: the standalone `remittance_cancel` escape-hatch check at the very top, the `matchesKnownCommand` escape-hatch list inside the `awaiting_remittance_last5` branch, and the five main dispatch `if` blocks. A disabled group's keywords landing on a customer's message now falls all the way through to the same silent "anything else" branch as a message matching no keyword at all — no reply of any kind, exactly as if that group's keywords were simply deleted (but the word list itself stays intact in the backend for whenever it's re-enabled).
- `GET`/`POST /api/backend/community/livestream-keywords` — same auth-guard shape as the other backend routes in this feature. `GET` now also returns `enabled` + `defaultsEnabled` alongside `keywords`/`defaults`. `POST` sanitizes each group (non-empty strings, capped length/count), separately accepts an `enabled` object in the body (passed straight through to `saveLivestreamKeywords`'s own sanitizer rather than re-validated at the route layer — any non-boolean or missing group defaults to `true`), and before saving runs `findDuplicateKeywords()` — an **exact-match** (case-insensitive) check for the same keyword appearing in more than one group, unaffected by enable/disable state (a disabled group's keywords still count for this collision check, since it's warning about the word list itself, not about what currently fires). If any collisions are found and the request didn't pass `force: true`, it responds `{ ok:false, needsConfirm:true, duplicates:[...] }` instead of erroring outright — this is a soft warning, not a hard block, per instructions ("跳個提示讓管理員自己決定要不要繼續存"). The route loops over `LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUPS` to build the incoming payload, so it never needed touching across any of these group additions/removals.
- Backend UI: a "關鍵字設定" toolbar button (社群連線訂單 section, alongside 收款帳號設定) opens a modal with one textarea per group (下單/匯款/取消/查詢訂單/傳照片確認/取消匯款), one keyword per line or comma-separated — the frontend's own load/save/duplicate-check logic already iterated `Object.keys(COMMUNITY_LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUP_LABELS)` generically, so adding/removing a group only ever needed a textarea + a map entry, no new JS logic. On save, if the API responds `needsConfirm`, the frontend shows a `confirm()` listing which keyword collides across which groups (in Chinese group names) and, if confirmed, resubmits with `force:true` — no separate "are you sure" UI component, just the browser's own `confirm()`. **Each group's textarea now has an "啟用中"/"已停用" pill toggle button sitting next to its label (this round)**: clicking it flips a `data-enabled` attribute on the button itself plus the button's text/color and the textarea's opacity (`.community-livestream-keyword-textarea-disabled`) — the button's `dataset.enabled` is the single source of truth read back on save, mirroring how the textareas themselves have never needed a separate JS state object. Disabling a group only dims its textarea and is purely cosmetic for the admin — the textarea is never given the `disabled` attribute, so the word list stays fully editable while off; it's a "does this trigger for customers" switch, not an "can I manage this list" switch.
- The `cancel` group (功能六's trigger keywords) was included in the same round the other 3 original groups were made configurable, even though the request only explicitly asked about the other three — the user asked "順便問一下...建議一起做" and confirmed doing all four then rather than opening a separate round later for it.
- **`edit_nickname` group removed in an earlier round** (the "修改暱稱" feature it backed was deleted along with 功能一's binding/review mechanism). **`done`/`quantity_confirm` removed in a later round**: the old "好了" quantity-confirmation screen and "數量正確" lock mechanism they triggered were both deleted entirely — those two groups and their backend-settings textareas were deleted rather than left as dead UI. **`photo_confirm` added in a later round** (default `傳好了`/`好了`/`傳完了`/`完成`) — note this reuses similar-sounding default keywords to the old, removed `done` group, but the semantics are completely different: it doesn't bring back any quantity-confirmation screen, it's a much simpler "did you actually receive my photos" check (see 功能二 below). **`remittance_cancel` added this round** (default `取消匯款`) — a deliberate "escape hatch" keyword for a customer stuck in the `awaiting_remittance_last5` state (see 功能五 below for why this was needed and a real substring-matching bug it exposed). Keyword groups have gone from 6 (at their peak) → 5 → 3 → 4 → 5 → **6** (this round): `order`/`remittance`/`cancel`/`order_query`/`photo_confirm`/`remittance_cancel`. **The prior round's `查詢訂單`-as-a-`cancel`-alias approach was wrong and is corrected this round**: `查詢訂單` is pulled back out of `cancel`'s default list and given its own new `order_query` group (default just `查詢訂單`) — see 功能六/七 below for why "query my orders" and "cancel an order" turned out to need genuinely different query logic (no status filter + no button, vs. a `not_bought`/`unpaid` filter + a cancel button), not just two names for the same list. Confirmed via a local substring-overlap simulation that `查詢訂單` doesn't cross-match any other group's default keywords (the same class of bug `remittance_cancel` hit two rounds ago), so `handleTextMessage`'s check order didn't need restructuring for this addition.
- Not yet manually tested (needs the pending migrations, same blocker as everything else in this feature) — see the updated manual test list below.

### Configurable reply templates — 16 customer-facing messages

The main-flow reply texts a customer actually sees (下單 welcome, photo-confirm, cancel, remittance — **not** the rare internal error/edge-case strings like "圖片下載失敗"/"查詢訂單失敗", which stay hardcoded on purpose) are backend-editable, **no new migration**, same `schedule_settings` generic-KV pattern as everything else in this feature:

- `src/lib/line/livestream-reply-templates.ts` — `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_REPLY_TEMPLATES` holds all 16 keys' default text, byte-for-byte identical to what's actually sent, so a backend that's never touched the settings screen behaves exactly as before. `getLivestreamReplyTemplates()` reads the admin override from `schedule_settings` (`type='community-livestream-reply-templates'`, JSON in `image`) and falls back to the defaults on any read failure or missing/blank per-key value (mirrors `livestream-keywords.ts`'s `sanitizeAllGroups` — an admin can't save an empty template, only overwrite or fall back). `renderLivestreamReplyTemplate(template, vars)` does simple `{{var}}` → value substitution, same placeholder syntax as `community-notification-templates.ts`'s `{{商品清單}}`.
- Templates are fetched **once per webhook event**, not once per message sent: `handleTextMessage`/`handleImageMessage`/`handlePostback` (the three entry points `handleLineEvent` dispatches to) each call `getLivestreamReplyTemplates()` exactly once at their top and thread the result down through every function they call (`handleCancelOrder`, `handlePhotoConfirmTrigger`, etc. all take a `templates` parameter) — same "query once per request, not per reply" discipline as `getLivestreamKeywords()`.
- The 16 keys, grouped: **下單/傳圖** — `order_welcome` (carries the "超過 10 分鐘沒上傳新照片請重新輸入「我要下單」" reminder), `photo_confirm_success`/`photo_confirm_empty`. **取消訂單** — `cancel_empty`/`cancel_intro_all`/`cancel_intro_partial`(`{{數量}}`)/`cancel_blocked_bought`/`cancel_blocked_paying`/`cancel_success`(`{{商品名稱}}`). **查詢訂單** — `order_query_empty`/`order_query_intro`(**new this round**, see 功能七 below). **匯款** — `remittance_empty`/`remittance_summary`(`{{收款資訊}}`)/`remittance_format_invalid`(now also mentions the `remittance_cancel` escape-hatch keyword, see 功能五 below)/`remittance_cancelled`/`remittance_success`.
- This feature's reply-template count has gone from a peak of 25 keys → 20 → 10 → 11 → 13 → 14 → **16** (this round, `order_query_empty`/`order_query_intro` added for the new 功能七). Any admin customization saved under a key that was removed in an earlier round is simply ignored (`sanitizeTemplates` only iterates the current `LIVESTREAM_REPLY_TEMPLATE_KEYS`) — harmless leftover data, no migration needed since this is a JSON blob not a schema.
- `GET`/`POST /api/backend/community/livestream-reply-templates` — same auth-guard shape as every other backend route in this feature; `POST` validates the key against `LIVESTREAM_REPLY_TEMPLATE_KEYS` and rejects an empty text body.
- Backend UI: a "訊息文案設定" toolbar button (社群連線訂單 section, next to 關鍵字設定) opens a settings-list modal (14 rows under 3 category headers — `remittance_cancelled` joined the existing "匯款" category rather than getting its own — each row has a truncated preview + "編輯訊息" button) that reuses the exact same `.community-line-template-*` CSS classes the pre-existing LINE 通知文案設定 modal uses. Clicking 編輯訊息 opens a shared edit modal with a `<textarea>` (admin controls line breaks/paragraphs directly), a per-key set of "插入 {{變數}}" buttons (only shown for keys that actually have placeholders — `remittance_cancelled` has none), a "恢復預設文案" button, and 儲存/取消. Same pattern as the LINE 通知文案設定 modal, generalized for per-key variable sets instead of one fixed set of three.
- Not yet manually tested (needs the pending migrations, same blocker as everything else in this feature) — see the updated manual test list below.

### Not usable yet — 3 manual setup steps required

1. **Apply the 11 pending migrations to Staging** (see below) — nothing in this feature works at all until they're applied (every route degrades to a DB error, not a silent no-op, since these are brand-new tables/bucket with no fallback).
2. **Set `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on Vercel** (Preview env, at minimum) — without it, `recognizeProductPhoto()` returns `null` immediately and every photo order row is created with `product_name`/`unit_price`/`recognized_confidence` all null (the "無法辨識價格" bucket), which is the intended graceful-degradation behavior but means recognition simply won't work until this key exists. `LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET` is **already set** on Preview (confirmed via `vercel env ls`, set ~6 days before this round — pre-existing, not something this round configured), so webhook signature verification is already live as soon as migrations are applied.
3. **Register the webhook URL in the LINE Developers console**: `https://pinkkkuin-staging.vercel.app/api/line/webhook`, on the same Messaging API channel `LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN`/`LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET` already belong to (the one push notifications already use). Also recommended: configure a Rich Menu with a "我要下單" button — either a `message` action that sends the literal text "我要下單", or a `postback` action (both are matched the same way, see below) — LINE Rich Menu configuration itself is outside this codebase, done in the LINE console/API directly.

Confirmed via `curl -X POST https://pinkkkuin-staging.vercel.app/api/line/webhook`: returns `401` (not `404`), meaning `isLineWebhookConfigured()` already finds `LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET` and the signature check correctly rejects an unsigned test request — the route itself is live, just untested end-to-end (needs a real signed request from LINE, i.e. step 3 above).

### ⚠️ Rich Menu — do not publish to all friends yet

This is a **real** LINE Official Account with **1,546 existing friends**. The new "我要下單" Rich Menu must **not** be set as the account's default/全體 menu until testing is done — every one of those friends would immediately see and be able to trigger the new ordering flow.

- Create the Rich Menu (image + tappable areas + the "我要下單" action) via the LINE console or the Rich Menu API as usual — creating it does **not** by itself show it to anyone.
- **Do not** call `POST /v2/bot/user/all/richmenu/{richMenuId}` (set-as-default-for-all) and **do not** flip the console's "Set as default rich menu" toggle while testing.
- Instead, link it to **one test LINE user id at a time** via `linkRichMenuToUser`:
  ```
  POST https://api.line.me/v2/bot/user/{testUserId}/richmenu/{richMenuId}
  Authorization: Bearer <LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN>
  ```
  (no request body). Only that one user id then sees the new menu; everyone else keeps whatever menu (or none) they already had.
- To remove it from a test user afterward: `DELETE https://api.line.me/v2/bot/user/{testUserId}/richmenu` (same auth header).
- A ready-to-use helper script was added for this: `scripts/line-link-rich-menu-to-user.js` — reads `LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN` from `.env`/`.env.local`/the environment (same token `src/lib/line/client.ts` already uses).
  ```
  node scripts/line-link-rich-menu-to-user.js <richMenuId> <testLineUserId>
  node scripts/line-link-rich-menu-to-user.js --unlink <testLineUserId>
  ```
- Only switch the Rich Menu to "set as default for all" once the full flow (photo upload → backend view → self-service remittance/cancel — **no binding/nickname step, no quantity-confirmation/price-dispute screen anymore, see 功能一/二 above**) has been manually verified end-to-end using this test-user-only link.

Handles the enum-narrowing safely (rename old type → build new 2-value type → convert the column, mapping any existing `confirming` rows to `not_bought` → drop the old type → rename the new one into place), does **not** assume Staging has no `confirming` rows left over from testing. **Not yet run against any database.**

- `supabase/migrations/202609300003_community_livestream_orders_delete_grant.sql` — `grant delete on public.community_livestream_orders to service_role;`. The original `202609290001` migration only granted `select, insert, update` — hard-delete (backend delete button + 功能六's self-cancel flow) needs the `delete` grant to actually work at the DB level, not just in application code. **Not yet run against any database.**

The customer-facing "價格有誤" reporting flow this backed was **removed this round** (see 功能二 below) — columns left as-is, unused. **Not yet run against any database.**

Originally added so the remittance-report flow could skip writing an amount entirely; this round's "self-reported amount" flow (see 功能五 below) now always supplies a value, but the column staying nullable is still correct/harmless. Pure constraint relaxation, no data loss, no type change. **Not yet run against any database.**
- `supabase/migrations/202610030001_community_livestream_remittances_bank_name.sql` — `alter table community_livestream_remittances add column if not exists bank_name text` — the customer's self-reported bank name from the fixed 3-line remittance format (see 功能五 below), stored alongside the existing `account_last5`/`amount` columns for the admin to cross-check. Purely additive, no grant changes needed. **Not yet run against any database.**
- `supabase/migrations/202610040001_community_livestream_remittances_review.sql` — grants `update, delete` on `community_livestream_remittances` to `service_role` (the table was deliberately append-only — `select, insert` only — until now; the new 後台「匯款紀錄」頁籤, 功能八 below, needs to actually edit/delete rows), and adds `reviewed_at timestamptz` (null = not yet reviewed by an admin, a value = reviewed) backing that tab's 未核對/已核對 quick filters. Purely additive + a grant widening, no data migration, no type change. **Not yet run against any database.**
- `supabase/migrations/202610050001_community_livestream_product_number_counter.sql` — **new this round**: adds `next_product_number integer not null default 1` to `community_line_bot_states`, and creates `increment_livestream_product_number(p_line_user_id text) returns integer` — a `security definer` SQL function doing an atomic `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE ... RETURNING` (a single statement, so Postgres serializes concurrent calls against the same row automatically — no two calls can ever read-then-write the same counter value). Backs `generateFallbackProductName`'s fix for a real race condition — see 功能二 below. `execute` granted to `service_role`. **Not yet run against any database.**
- All 11 need to be applied manually in the Staging SQL Editor, same process as every other pending migration in this project, **in this order** (each later one depends on an earlier one already existing). Until applied: the webhook still returns 200 to LINE for every event (so LINE won't retry-storm), but every DB call inside the handler fails silently (caught, logged, no reply sent) — so from the customer's side, the bot would just never respond. The backend's 社群連線訂單/匯款紀錄 tabs' GET/PATCH/DELETE/import routes will all return their generic 500 error, and the 收款帳號設定/批次修改/刪除/取消訂單/關鍵字設定 UI pieces have nothing to operate on. `generateFallbackProductName`'s RPC call will also fail until `202610050001` specifically is applied — see its own fallback behavior in 功能二 below.

Expand the accordion → confirm it shows that customer's order cards looking and behaving **identically** to the flat view (same thumbnail/time/name/已購買 toggle/qty+price inputs/status pills/編輯/刪除) — edit the quantity or unit price on one of these cards and confirm it autosaves locally (no flicker) exactly like in the flat view; confirm the accordion header's 4 amounts do **not** update immediately after that edit (expected staleness, not a bug — see 功能九 above) but **do** update after clicking 重新整理 or toggling 依客人統計 off and back on. Toggle 依客人統計 off → confirm it reverts to the normal flat, time-sorted list with no leftover grouping artifacts. Turn on 批次編輯 while 依客人統計 is also on → confirm staged (unsaved) quantity/price edits inside an expanded accordion group show the same amber dirty-border + "保存全部修改（已改 N 筆）" behavior as the flat view, and that saving them works the same way.
 (ab) **new this round — 匯款紀錄人名→匯款紀錄雙層結構 (功能八)**: open 匯款紀錄 → confirm each customer's name is always shown with their remittances listed directly underneath (no click needed to see them), and confirm a customer who has placed orders but never self-reported any remittance appears in the list showing **only** a "未匯款" row (系統計算總額 matching their actual unpaid-order total) and nothing else. For a customer with at least one `confirming`/reviewed remittance and also at least one currently-`unpaid` order, confirm both their remittance row(s) **and** a trailing "未匯款" row appear, in that order. Click a remittance row's expand chevron → confirm it shows that remittance's covered order(s) as photo+name+qty×price+subtotal rows, matching what's actually in `order_ids`; collapse it again. For a remittance whose customer-reported `amount` doesn't match the row's own "系統" total, confirm the ⚠ badge appears next to the amount input; edit the amount to match and confirm the badge disappears after the reload. Edit a remittance's 末五碼/金額 inline → confirm it autosaves (full reload, not local-patch) and the value persists. Use the 銀行 dropdown on a remittance whose current value is one of 中信/富邦/國泰 → confirm the dropdown shows and lets you change it among exactly those 3 options; for a remittance with an "異常" bank name (not matching any alias), confirm the dropdown shows a 4th, pre-selected "⚠ {actual value}" option so the current (wrong) value is visible rather than silently defaulting to 中信 — then actually pick 中信/富邦/國泰 from the dropdown to fix it and confirm the row's ⚠ anomaly indicator clears on reload. Click the 中信/富邦/國泰/異常/全部 filter buttons one at a time → confirm exactly one is highlighted at a time and the customer list narrows to those with **at least one** matching remittance (not just their single latest one) — test specifically with a customer who reported one 中信 and one 富邦 remittance: confirm they appear under **both** the 中信 and 富邦 filters, not just one. Click 異常 → confirm it surfaces customers with at least one unmatched bank name. Search by a partial customer name → confirm it narrows correctly. As the actual regression case for the alias table: have a test customer report one remittance as "中國信託" and another as "中信" (two separate 我要匯款 → reply flows) → confirm **both** rows display as "中信" (not two different-looking rows) and both show up under the 中信 filter. Click 標記已核對/取消標記 and 刪除 (with its `confirm()`) on individual remittance rows → confirm both still work exactly as before. Confirm the overall customer ordering follows: 中信 customers (newest remittance first) → 富邦 → 國泰 → 異常 → then customers with zero remittances at all, alphabetically. (ac) **new this round**: as a test LINE user, trigger "我要匯款" to enter the `awaiting_remittance_last5` state, then copy the exact prompt text the bot just sent (the `remittance_summary` message, which contains the literal word "完成" or "匯款" depending on current template wording) and paste it back with the bank/amount/last-5 answers filled in inline on the same lines (e.g. appending the actual values after each "：") → confirm this is accepted as a valid report (`remittance_success` reply, order `payment_status` flips to `confirming`) rather than being silently discarded or treated as an escape command. Separately, re-confirm the existing escape-hatch behavior still works for messages that are **only** a known command word with no parseable report mixed in (e.g. plain "取消訂單", "傳好了") — these should still correctly clear the waiting state and run their own flow, not get stuck trying to parse them as a report. In the backend's 匯款紀錄 tab, click 新增匯款紀錄 → select a customer with unpaid orders → confirm their unpaid orders list appears with all checkboxes unchecked by default → click 全選 → confirm all check → uncheck one individually → confirm it stays unchecked while the rest remain checked → fill in 銀行/末五碼/金額 → submit → confirm the new row appears under that customer in the list, and that the orders you left checked (not the one you unchecked) flipped to `confirming` while the unchecked one is still `unpaid`. Try submitting with no customer selected, no order checked, and an invalid 末五碼 → confirm each is rejected with a clear error without submitting anything. Click 匯出 Excel with at least a few real remittance records loaded → open the file and confirm one row per actual remittance (not per order, not including the "未匯款" pseudo-row) with correctly-encoded Traditional Chinese headers/values and 系統計算金額/是否核對/匯款時間 matching what the tab shows on screen. (ad) **new this round**: open 關鍵字設定 — confirm each of the 6 groups now shows an "啟用中" pill next to its label by default (first time opening, or against never-saved data). Toggle one group (e.g. 取消) to "已停用" — confirm its textarea visually dims but the keyword text inside it is still fully editable (type/delete freely) — then save. As a test LINE user, send each of that group's default keywords ("取消訂單"/"我要取消"/"取消") → confirm **zero reply of any kind** for all three, same as sending gibberish; confirm every other still-enabled group (我要下單/我要匯款/查詢訂單/傳好了/取消匯款) still triggers normally. Re-open 關鍵字設定 → confirm it correctly reloads showing 取消 as "已停用" (not reset back to enabled) — toggle it back to "啟用中" and save → confirm "取消訂單" etc. work again immediately. Separately, trigger "我要匯款" to enter the `awaiting_remittance_last5` state, disable the `order` group, save, then (while still in that waiting state) send "我要下單" → confirm it does **not** escape the waiting state anymore (since `order` is disabled, it no longer counts as a known command) and instead falls through to being parsed as a malformed report (`remittance_format_invalid` reply) rather than silently clearing the state — re-enable `order` afterward. Finally, confirm a backend that has never opened this settings screen at all behaves exactly as before this round (all 6 groups effectively enabled) — this can be checked by reading the fallback path in `getLivestreamKeywordSettings`/`parseStoredSettings`, since there's no way to actually reset `schedule_settings` back to "never saved" from the UI once something's been saved once.

### 功能一：下單觸發 — **暱稱綁定/審核機制已移除（this round）**

- **這道關卡整個拿掉了**：原本客人要先申請社群暱稱、等後台「社群名單」審核通過才能用下單/取消/匯款/數量確認任何功能；重新評估後決定移除——真正區分客人身份的本來就是 `line_user_id`（LINE 帳號本身的唯一識別碼，沒辦法偽造），暱稱只是疊加上去給人看的標籤，審核這關沒有真正買到安全性，只是增加客人下單前的門檻。現在**客人第一次互動就能直接使用所有功能**。
- `src/lib/line/livestream-orders.ts::handleOrderTrigger`（「我要下單」觸發，`order` 關鍵字組）現在只是單純回覆功能二的歡迎文字，不再查任何 binding 狀態、不再有 pending/approved/未綁定三種分支。
- `handleImageMessage`/`handleDoneCommand`/`handleCancelTrigger`/`handleRemittanceTrigger` 這些入口原本都有 `getBinding`/`isApprovedBinding` 檢查、沒通過就回 `not_bound_yet` 文案擋下——**這些檢查跟 `not_bound_yet` 文案本身都已經移除**，直接放行。
- `community_livestream_orders` 建立資料列時（`handleImageMessage` 裡），新增的 `getLineDisplayName(userId)` 透過既有的 `getLineUserProfile` 直接拿客人**當下**的 LINE 顯示名稱，同時寫入 `nickname` 跟 `line_display_name` 兩個欄位（兩者值相同）——不再依賴 `community_line_bindings` 查 nickname。`generateFallbackProductName`（自動商品命名「{LINE顯示名稱}-商品N」）也改成直接吃這個 `displayName` 字串參數，不用再傳整個 binding 物件。`handleRemittanceLast5Submission` 寫入 `community_livestream_remittances.nickname` 同樣改用這個 helper。
- **「修改暱稱」功能整個移除**：`handleEditNicknameTrigger`/`handleNicknameSubmission`/`startNicknameRequest` 連同暱稱撞名檢查都已刪除，`edit_nickname` 關鍵字組也從 `LIVESTREAM_KEYWORD_GROUPS`/後台「關鍵字設定」畫面移除——沒有自訂暱稱這件事了，這個功能失去意義。打「修改暱稱」現在就跟任何其他無法辨識的文字一樣，落入 `handleTextMessage` 結尾「intentionally silent」的分支，沒有任何回覆。
- `community_line_bot_states.awaiting_nickname` 欄位**不再讀寫**（`BotState` type/`getBotState`/`setBotState` 都已移除這個欄位），但 migration 沒有特別砍掉這個欄位，留著無害。
- **明確沒動的部分**：`community_line_bindings` 這張表、後台「社群名單」審核頁面、`/api/backend/community/members` 這支 API **完全沒碰**——這些是給另一套舊的記事本/社群訂單系統用的，跟這次調整無關；本功能從頭到尾不曾、也不再讀寫這張表。

### 功能二：LINE 圖片下單對話流程

- Opening message on "我要下單": `已開啟下單功能，請直接上傳您要的商品圖片即可，不需要再輸入其他文字！提醒您：若超過 10 分鐘沒有上傳新照片，請重新輸入「我要下單」繼續使用。`（backend-editable via `order_welcome` — see "Configurable reply templates" above；10 分鐘提醒句是**這輪新加的**，管理員自己改掉這句文字也完全沒問題）— 不需要任何綁定/審核（見功能一），任何 LINE 使用者都能立刻觸發。
- **Every image message** from any LINE user is: downloaded via `downloadLineMessageContent` (`GET https://api-data.line.me/v2/bot/message/{id}/content`), uploaded to the private `community-livestream-photos` bucket at `{lineUserId}/{messageId}.jpg`, run through `recognizeProductPhoto`（Anthropic vision）+ 免費 OCR 備援（見下方，完全沒改動），然後存成一筆 `community_livestream_orders`（`quantity: 1`、`source: 'line'`、`nickname`/`line_display_name` 來自 `getLineDisplayName`，見功能一）——**上傳本身永遠零回覆**（見下方「客人端數量/價格互動整組移除」）；客人要主動打「傳好了」才會觸發下面的確認信號，不是每張照片都回。
- **Free white-box OCR price fallback** — `src/lib/line/price-ocr.ts::recognizePriceFromPhoto(buffer)`, called from `handleImageMessage` only when `recognizeProductPhoto`'s (Anthropic's) `price` came back `null` (currently: always, since `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` still isn't set anywhere — see "Not usable yet" above). Zero API cost, two new deps (`sharp` for pixel work, `tesseract.js` for OCR): (1) downscales the photo, scans pixels for high-purity-white (`R/G/B` all ≥225 and within 20 of each other — deliberately stricter than "looks white to the eye" to avoid matching overexposed backgrounds), flood-fills to find **every** connected white region above a 1%-of-image-area minimum (not just the single largest one — see the multi-box bullet below for why); (2) crops **each** candidate region independently (with ~8% padding) from the *original*-resolution image, upscales if small, and OCRs it with a shared `tesseract.js` worker; (3) reads the OCR result **line by line** (not as one text blob) and keeps a line as a price candidate only if, after trimming and stripping commas, it's purely digits. Exactly one numeric candidate across all boxes/lines → used as `unit_price`; zero or more than one (not found / genuinely ambiguous) → `null`, same "don't guess" contract as the Anthropic path, and it never overwrites a price Anthropic already found. The `tesseract.js` worker is created once and kept alive for the life of the warm serverless instance (`cachePath: '/tmp'`, no `.terminate()` call, no character whitelist — see below for why) rather than spun up per photo, to avoid repeatedly paying its WASM-init + language-data-download cost; when a photo has multiple white-box candidates, those are OCR'd **sequentially** against that one shared worker (not `Promise.all`), since a single `tesseract.js` worker can't run two `recognize()` calls concurrently.
- **Handles multiple/touching white boxes, not just the single largest one**: the original version only looked at the largest connected white region and picked the longest digit-run out of its whole OCR text blob. That broke when a photo had a second white-backed sticker near the price (e.g. a Chinese description like "是一個盤子") close enough that flood-fill merged both into **one** connected white region — the whole-blob text search then had digits and Chinese text mixed together with no clean way to separate them. Fixed by (a) collecting **all** sufficiently-large white regions instead of just the largest, so genuinely separate stickers are each OCR'd on their own, and (b) dropping `tessedit_char_whitelist` entirely and instead reading `data.blocks → paragraphs → lines` (tesseract.js's structured per-line output — note this must be explicitly requested via `recognize(image, {}, { blocks: true })`, since `blocks` is `false` in tesseract.js's own default output options) and keeping only lines that are purely numeric after trimming — a Chinese-text line now reads as itself (not force-mapped into digit guesses) and gets correctly excluded, rather than contaminating a single merged OCR result. A photo with two *separate* boxes that are each cleanly numeric but disagree (genuine ambiguity) still correctly returns `null` rather than guessing between them.
- **Tested against the real 4 reference photos — 2 real bugs found and fixed**: the user dropped the actual reference photos into `scratch/price-ocr-samples/` (gitignored, never committed) along with a ready-made test script (`scratch/test-price-ocr.ts`, kept as-is for anyone to rerun the same way — see "Local verification without LINE/webhook" below for how). Before that round's fixes, **0 of the 4 real photos** were recognized correctly (all returned `null`) despite 3 of them having large, unambiguous white price stickers — visually inspecting the actual crops (saved to disk via a throwaway debug script) found two distinct root causes: (1) real stickers have **rounded corners**, so the ~8% crop padding occasionally pulled in a sliver of background at a corner, which Tesseract read as one stray non-letter symbol tacked onto the real digits (`"280 °"`, `"270 |"`) — the line-purity check required the *entire* line to be digits/commas, so this silently discarded an otherwise-correct reading. Fixed by stripping any character that isn't a letter, digit, or comma before the purity check (`extractNumericLineValue`) — a real description line still gets rejected exactly as before, since it still contains actual letters; only non-alphabetic noise is now tolerated. (2) One crop — a clean, isolated "280" on white with nothing else in it — made Tesseract's **default page-segmentation mode (PSM AUTO)** return completely empty text; testing every PSM value in isolation found PSM 11 (`SPARSE_TEXT`) reads it perfectly. Now retries the *same* crop once with PSM 11 only when the default PSM found zero lines (then resets PSM back to `AUTO` for the next crop) — deliberately kept as a fallback rather than the default, since PSM 11 is more prone to shattering a busier crop (e.g. the touching-stickers case) into misread fragments; confirmed via testing that switching to PSM 11 globally actually makes the touching-stickers photo *worse* (misreads `990` as `9290` as a clean-looking but wrong standalone line) — the targeted-fallback approach avoids that regression entirely. **Result: 3 of the 4 real photos now correctly recognized (280/270/280)**; the 4th (the exact "two touching white stickers, price + Chinese description merged into one connected region" case the multi-box support above was built for) still correctly returns `null` — in that specific photo, OCR reads the price digits fused with stray letters on the very same text line (not a separate line), which the line-purity rule correctly treats as too ambiguous to trust rather than guessing.
- **Two real bugs found and fixed building the original version** (both discovered via a synthetic sanity test built locally — see "Verification" below): (1) the crop pipeline originally called `sharp().greyscale()` before OCR "to help it read cleaner" — that actually made `tesseract.js` return empty text 100% of the time in this stack (`.greyscale()` outputs a single-channel PNG, which this version of `tesseract.js`/Leptonica silently fails to read); removed it, `.normalise()` alone is enough contrast boost and doesn't break anything. (2) `sharp` conflicted with the `sharp@0.34.5` Next.js already depends on internally (for `next/image`) — installing our own `^0.35.5` left two different `sharp` versions in the tree, which on Vercel's Node runtime produced `ERR_DLOPEN_FAILED: libvips-cpp.so.8.18.7` at request time (crashed the whole webhook with a `500`, not just the OCR call) even though a completely fresh, uncached `npm install` had run; fixed by pinning our `sharp` to the exact `0.34.5` Next already uses successfully in production, which dedupes to one copy (confirmed via `npm ls sharp --all`). See "Deployment notes" below for the full debugging trail — this is a real trap worth remembering for any future dependency that might also want `sharp`.
- **客人端數量/價格互動整組移除（this round）**：延續上一輪移除暱稱綁定的簡化方向，這輪把整個「傳照片之後」的對話互動都拿掉——客人現在只剩「傳照片」「我要匯款」「取消訂單」三件事，數量/價格完全交給後台人工處理。整組移除、不留殘餘半套邏輯：
  - 「好了」觸發詞/keyword group、`handleDoneCommand`，連帶原本的「單輪最多 10 張」上傳上限（`countPendingPhotos`、`photo_over_cap` 文案）——沒有「好了」批次顯示這個概念了，這個上限本來就只是為了限制一次顯示多少張；拿掉觸發點後如果繼續保留，會變成一個永遠無法解除的卡住警告，所以一併移除，不是半套殘留。`MAX_PHOTOS_PER_ROUND`（常數本身，值仍是 10）**沒有刪**，現在單純當作功能六取消訂單 Carousel 的批次大小在用，跟上傳無關
  - 數量確認清單本身：`buildOrderItemBlock`（縮圖+名稱+單價/數量文字+1/2/3/4/5+ 按鈕+「價格有誤」「刪除」按鈕的那個 item block）整支刪除；`applyQuantityUpdate`（1/2/3/4 按鈕 + 5+ 輸入數字共用的寫入函式）整支刪除；`carousel_sent_at` 欄位不再寫入/比對（欄位留著不用）
  - 「價格有誤」回報整套：`handlePriceDisputeSubmission`、`awaiting_price_dispute_for_order_id`（bot state 欄位，程式碼不再讀寫，DB 欄位留著）、`parsePriceAmount`
  - 清單上的「刪除」按鈕：`handleDeletePendingOrder`
  - 「數量正確」觸發詞/keyword group、`handleQuantityConfirmTrigger`、`quantity_locked_at` 鎖定判斷——`applyQuantityUpdate` 本身都刪了，鎖定判斷自然跟著消失；`quantity_locked_at` 欄位留著不用
  - `buildOrderListBubble`（把 item block 陣列組成一個 Flex bubble 的純容器函式）**沒有刪**——雖然原本唯一的呼叫者（`handleDoneCommand`）沒了，但功能五的匯款清單繼續沿用這支函式，所以留著
  - 背景的 `recognizeProductPhoto`/`recognizePriceFromPhoto` 自動辨識**完全沒有改動**——商品名稱/單價還是照常嘗試辨識並存進資料庫，只是客人端再也看不到、也不能調整這些值，純粹是後台管理員自己看、自己編輯
- **「傳好了」確認信號 — 曾經短暫加過自動清單，這輪改回純文字（across three rounds）**：客人打 `photo_confirm` 關鍵字組（預設「傳好了」/「好了」/「傳完了」/「完成」，後台可設定）時，`handlePhotoConfirmTrigger` 查這個 `line_user_id` 最近 `RECENT_PHOTO_WINDOW_MINUTES`（常數值 10，**跟 `order_welcome` 文案裡「超過 10 分鐘沒上傳新照片」用的是同一個時間概念**，純文字提醒跟這裡的真實查詢窗各自獨立存在，改其中一個要記得手動保持另一個同步，程式碼沒有互相引用）分鐘內有幾筆 `community_livestream_orders`——**這輪改回輕量 `count` 查詢**（`select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })`），不選 `product_name`/`photo_storage_path`，純粹拿來判斷要回哪句文字，不抓資料來顯示。沒收到就回 `photo_confirm_empty`（預設提示先傳照片）；有收到就回 `photo_confirm_success`（預設文字改成「已錄入商品，感謝您的訂購！如想查詢目前訂單，請輸入「查詢訂單」。」，比前一輪多了提示句）。**中間有一輪曾經在 `photo_confirm_success` 文字後面多送一則 Flex 清單**，把剛收到的商品用共用的圖片+商品名稱樣式列出來——這輪再改回去，理由是客人不一定想每次都看到清單，改成純文字+提示，清單改成客人自己打「查詢訂單」才會跳出來（見下面功能六）。跟被整組移除的舊版「好了」數量確認清單在語意上仍然完全不同（沒有按鈕、沒有數量/價格、不能互動，純粹是一句確認文字）。
- **`generateFallbackProductName` 的競態條件修正（this round）**：原本是「查詢這個 `line_user_id` 目前有幾筆 `community_livestream_orders` + 1」算編號——客人連續快速傳好幾張照片時，多個 `handleImageMessage` 呼叫會在前一筆真正寫進資料庫之前幾乎同時查到同一個筆數，算出同一個編號，造成兩筆不同訂單撞號（都叫「商品3」）。改呼叫新的 `increment_livestream_product_number` RPC（`202610050001` migration，見上面「Blocked」）——內部是單一 SQL 陳述式的原子遞增，不管同時有幾支呼叫平行打進來都不會拿到重複的號碼。**RPC 失敗時的備援刻意不是退回舊算法**（那正是這次要修掉的競態來源）：改用 `Date.now().toString(36)` + 隨機亂碼尾碼組一個「{LINE顯示名稱}-商品-{亂碼}」，犧牲編號的連續性/可讀性，換取任何情況下都不會撞名——這個備援路徑在 `202610050001` 套用之前（即現在）是**實際會走到的路徑**，不是純理論上的防禦，所以目前任何辨識失敗的商品名稱會長得像「{顯示名稱}-商品-m1a2b3xyz」而不是乾淨的「-商品3」，等 migration 套用後才會恢復成乾淨的遞增數字。
- **共用的「圖片+商品名稱」清單樣式（across three rounds — enlarged, then reverted to the old small-thumbnail size, then「傳好了」停止呼叫）**：`buildPhotoNameItemBlock(row)`（吃 `{ id, product_name, photo_storage_path }`）函式本身**沒有拿掉**，只是這輪「傳好了」改回純文字確認後不再呼叫它——目前唯一的呼叫者是功能五的匯款清單（`handleRemittanceTrigger`）。單一項目內部是**橫向排列**（`layout:"horizontal"`）——左邊是縮圖、右邊是商品名稱文字；這個 item block 本身再跟 `buildOrderListBubble` 的 `separator` 搭配組成一項接一項往下排的**直向清單**（不是橫向滑動的 Carousel；橫向排列只發生在單一項目內部）。**圖片尺寸這輪改回舊版大小**：原本一度放大成接近滿版的正方形大圖，這輪改回舊版（還有數量按鈕時）`buildOrderItemBlock` 用過的 `size:"60px"` 小縮圖，比照舊規格。沒有照片的訂單**直接省略圖片**，整列只剩商品名稱文字（不是之前那版的灰底佔位區塊——拿掉按鈕/數量後，單純漏掉圖片本身就不會顯示壞圖，不需要額外的佔位視覺）。

### 功能三：後台「社群連線訂單」頁籤

- New tab in the existing `lily-backend.html` (submodule), under 社群管理 → 社群連線訂單 — same backend, same deploy, no new site.
- **One table, not two**: `community_livestream_orders` is simultaneously the bot's "待確認佇列" and the final admin-visible order list — a photo becomes a row the moment it's recognized (`quantity` defaulting to 1, `purchase_status='not_bought'`, `payment_status='unpaid'`), and the LINE quantity-buttons/typed-number flow just updates that same row's `quantity` in place. There was never a need for a separate staging table.
- Fields: LINE 名稱, 社群暱稱, 下單商品 (editable text input), 數量 (editable number input), 單價 (editable number input, blank = null/unrecognized), 總價 (`total_price`, a **generated column** — `unit_price * quantity`, or null if `unit_price` is null — never an input, purely computed/read-only in both the DB and the UI), 購買狀態 (未購買/已購買 dropdown — **narrowed to 2 values this round**, see below), 匯款狀態 (未付款/確認中/已付款 dropdown — **unchanged, still 3 values**, the `confirming` state is what 功能五's self-service remittance flow sets), 備註 (editable text input). Every edit is a `PATCH /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/[id]` call, one field at a time (same "onchange autosave" pattern used everywhere else in this admin).
- **購買狀態 simplified to two states this round** (`202609300001` migration, not yet applied — see above): `COMMUNITY_LIVESTREAM_PURCHASE_OPTIONS` in `lily-backend.html` now only lists 未購買/已購買, the "購買確認中" quick-filter button was removed (7 buttons → 6: 未購買/已購買/未付款/匯款確認中/已付款/無法辨識價格), and both API routes' `PURCHASE_STATUSES` validation sets were narrowed to `{'not_bought','bought'}`. 匯款狀態 was deliberately left at 3 states because 功能五 needs the `confirming` middle state.
- Status dropdowns reuse the existing `communityStatusSelect`-style visual (a `<select>` with a tone-colored class), but through **new, separate** functions `communityLivestreamStatusSelect`/`communityLivestreamStatusTone` — the existing `communityStatusTone` is hardcoded into `communityStatusSelect`'s `onchange` string (not parameterized), and its red/green mapping is tuned for the *old* system's status values (e.g. `unpaid` → green there); reusing it as-is for this feature's `unpaid`/`paid`/`not_bought`/`bought` values would show backwards colors. The old function/table are completely untouched.
- Mobile-first card list (`.community-livestream-card`, one order per card — a thumbnail + editable fields stacked vertically), not a table with horizontal scroll, per spec.
- **Summary/expand card layout + edit/delete icons**: every card always shows the same top area (a thumbnail with the batch checkbox overlaid — see the enlarged-image bullet below for its current size/layout — nickname/LINE display name, then 編輯/刪除 icon-only buttons pushed to the right by the middle block's `flex:1`) — this row never changes. Below it, exactly one of two blocks shows: by default `.community-livestream-summary` (product name as plain text — empty string if there isn't one, no special warning styling, see below — one combined `數量 × [單價 input] = 總價` line with small colored 購買/匯款狀態 pills — `communityLivestreamMiniPill`, reusing `communityLivestreamStatusTone` — and a 備註 line only if notes exist), or — after clicking 編輯 — `.community-livestream-full` (the full editable form: product name input, 數量/單價/總價 grid, the two status `<select>`s, notes input). Toggling is `toggleCommunityLivestreamCard(id)`, which just flips an `expanded` class on the card by id — same "collapsed by default, `data-row-id` + `.open`-style class toggle" technique as 記事本管理/出貨申請's detail rows, adapted from a table row to a card (CSS shows/hides `.community-livestream-summary` vs `.community-livestream-full` based on the card's own class, not a second DOM row). Which cards are expanded survives every re-render (read back from `.community-livestream-card.expanded` before rebuilding the list, same pattern as `communityNotebookRowId`/`openIds`). 刪除 (`deleteCommunityLivestreamOrder`) asks `confirm()` then calls `DELETE /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/[id]` (see below) — no separate safe-delete flow, this table is hard-deleted directly.
- **No "⚠ 未辨識" warning on an empty product name (changed this round)**: the summary's product-name line used to render a red "⚠ 未辨識" span when `productName` was falsy; that's gone now — an empty name just renders as empty text (`escapeHtml(order.productName || '')`). This case is expected to essentially never happen going forward (功能二's `generateFallbackProductName` guarantees a non-null name for every new LINE-sourced row — see 功能二 above); any row still showing blank is leftover pre-fix test data, not a new bug, but the warning UI itself was removed anyway so a future null (for whatever reason) doesn't visually alarm anyone.
- **Photo enlarged + left/right split layout + click-to-preview, now a fixed square (current state, across two rounds)**: `.community-livestream-card-top` is a 2-column CSS grid (`grid-template-columns: 110px 1fr; align-items: stretch`) — left column is the photo, right column is `.community-livestream-card-main` holding everything that used to be inline: **the header's actual contents have changed since this bullet was written — see the card-reflow bullet below for the current shape** (nickname/time/編輯/刪除 are no longer in `.community-livestream-card-header` at all), the 匯款 info banner (the 價格異常 banner that used to sit alongside it was removed in a later round, see below), and the summary/full blocks, all stacked vertically. **The photo itself is a fixed square** (`width:100%; aspect-ratio:1/1`, `.community-livestream-thumb-wrap` given `align-self:start`) — an earlier version of this used `height:100%` so the image stretched to match whatever height the right column's content needed, which both visually distorted the photo into a non-square shape and made cards noticeably taller once the right column had a lot of content (remittance/dispute banners, full edit form, etc.); switching to `aspect-ratio` fixed both problems at once — the photo is always a clean 110×110 square pinned to the top of its column regardless of how tall the right side gets, with any leftover space below it just left blank (expected, not a bug). `.community-livestream-card-main`'s own gap was also trimmed slightly (`0.5rem` → `0.4rem`) as a minor follow-up tightening, though the square-photo fix was the dominant contributor to the "cards got taller" complaint, not accumulated gap/margin stacking (those were already fairly tight, 0.3–0.6rem, with no real doubling-up found on inspection). Same rules apply on mobile and desktop, no breakpoint distinction. The select-checkbox overlay sits just inside the photo's corner (`top:0.4rem;left:0.4rem`). Clicking the photo (only when a real `photoUrl` exists — the "no photo" placeholder isn't clickable) calls `openCommunityLivestreamPhotoPreview(photoUrl)`, which reuses the exact same `.modal-overlay`/`.modal-active` mechanism every other modal in this file already uses (`communityLivestreamPhotoPreviewModal`, just a header + a single `<img>`, no footer) — no new popup component. **One deliberate addition beyond copying the existing pattern**: this new modal also closes on a background click (`onclick="if (event.target === this) closeCommunityLivestreamPhotoPreview()"` on the overlay itself) — checked, and no *other* modal in this codebase actually supports closing on background click today (every one of them is `×`-button/footer-button only); this was added only to this one new modal as a deliberate, scoped UX enhancement for an image preview specifically, not a retrofit of the shared modal pattern itself.
- **單價 is always an editable input in the summary view, not just inside 編輯**: unlike every other field (product name/quantity/notes/statuses, still editable only after expanding), 單價 was pulled out into a small `<input type="number">` directly in the collapsed summary line (`.community-livestream-summary-price-input`) — the same field is also still editable from inside the expanded form (`.community-livestream-full-price-input`), both now calling the same handler. Reason: unit price (especially "無法辨識") is the field the admin corrects constantly, and having to expand the card first for that one field was the most-complained-about friction.
- **Unit-price edits no longer trigger a full list reload (changed this round)**: `patchCommunityLivestreamUnitPrice(id, rawValue)` replaced the generic `patchCommunityLivestreamOrder(...)` call for this one field specifically (every other field still goes through the generic path and its full `loadCommunityLivestreamOrders()` reload — deliberately left alone this round, since price was the one the user actually flagged as feeling bad to use). After a successful `PATCH`, it: updates the in-memory `communityLivestreamOrders` row's `unitPrice`/`totalPrice` (`quantity * unitPrice`, mirroring the DB's own generated-column formula) directly; repaints just that card's total-price text in both places it appears (`.community-livestream-summary-total` and `.community-livestream-total`) and keeps both price `<input>`s (summary + full form) in sync with each other, since editing either one used to leave the other showing a stale value until the next full re-render; and removes the full form's "⚠ 無法辨識價格" notice (`.community-livestream-price-warning`) once a price is actually entered. **One exception still forces a removal from the visible list**: if the "無法辨識價格" quick filter is active and this row's price just went from `null` to a real number, it no longer belongs in the filtered view — that row is spliced out of `communityLivestreamOrders` and its card element removed from the DOM directly (count text updated, selection state cleaned up), still without a full reload. No other field/filter combination triggers this removal path.
- **數量 is now also always an editable input in the summary view (this round)**, matching 單價's treatment above — a small `<input type="number">` sits right next to the price input in the collapsed summary line (`.community-livestream-summary-qty-input`), with a matching `.community-livestream-full-qty-input` inside the expanded form; both call the same new `patchCommunityLivestreamQuantity(id, rawValue)`, which mirrors `patchCommunityLivestreamUnitPrice` exactly — updates the in-memory row's `quantity`/`totalPrice`, repaints just that card's total-price text in both places, and keeps both quantity `<input>`s in sync, all without a full `loadCommunityLivestreamOrders()` reload. No "remove from filtered view" exception like unit price has (no quick filter keys off quantity).
- **New this round — 批次編輯 (bulk-edit) mode, deliberately separate from the existing 批次修改/批次刪除 (bulk-status/bulk-delete) tools below**: a toolbar toggle button (`#community-livestream-bulk-edit-toggle-btn`, swaps `btn-secondary`↔`btn-primary` and its own label between "批次編輯"/"結束批次編輯") flips `communityLivestreamBulkEditEnabled`. While on, the 數量/單價 inputs' `onchange` is rewired (at render time, via the onchange string the template literal builds, not a separate event-listener layer) to call `stageCommunityLivestreamFieldChange(id, field, value)` instead of the immediate-PATCH functions above — this stores the pending edit in a module-level `communityLivestreamBulkEditChanges` `Map<id, {quantity?, unitPrice?}>` and updates only that card's DOM (both qty/price inputs, the total-price text, and a `.community-livestream-field-dirty` class for the amber "unsaved" border) — nothing is sent to the server yet. A "保存全部修改" button (`#community-livestream-bulk-edit-save-btn`, hidden via `style.display` outside bulk-edit mode) shows a live "（已改 N 筆）" count and stays `disabled` at 0; `saveCommunityLivestreamBulkEditChanges` fires one `PATCH .../livestream-orders/[id]` per staged id via `Promise.all` — reusing the existing single-item endpoint, no new batch API, same technique as 批次修改 below — then clears the Map and does a full reload. Turning the toggle back off with pending changes triggers a `confirm()` ("目前有 N 筆尚未儲存的修改，確定要放棄嗎？") before actually discarding them and switching back to immediate-autosave mode. Naming was deliberately kept out of the `communityLivestreamBatch*` family (used by the pre-existing checkbox-select + apply-one-value-to-many tools) since this is a conceptually different "each row stages its own distinct edit" mechanism, not "apply one chosen value to several selected rows" — picking a colliding name would have made the two easy to confuse while reading the code.
- **New this round — 卡片右上角「已購買」快速打勾按鈕 + 購買狀態／付款狀態也改成免刷新局部更新**: a new icon-only toggle (`.community-livestream-purchase-toggle-btn`, leftmost of the three `.community-livestream-card-actions` buttons — 已購買/編輯/刪除) flips `purchaseStatus` between `not_bought`/`bought` with no confirm dialog, styled with a new `.community-livestream-icon-btn.active` rule (green fill/border/check, copied from the 匯款紀錄 tab's `.community-remittances-icon-btn.active` look — kept as its own separate CSS rule on this feature's own `.community-livestream-icon-btn` class rather than literally sharing the remittances one, same "visually matching, logically separate" convention as every other cross-tab style copy in this feature). The two status `<select>`s in the expanded form were rewired from the generic `patchCommunityLivestreamOrder(...)` (full reload) to a new shared `patchCommunityLivestreamStatus(id, field, value)` — mirrors `patchCommunityLivestreamUnitPrice`/`patchCommunityLivestreamQuantity`'s "PATCH, then touch only this card's DOM" shape, but is itself field-generic (`field` is `'purchaseStatus'` or `'paymentStatus'`) since both statuses need the exact same three-places-to-update behavior. `communityLivestreamMiniPill`/`communityLivestreamStatusSelect` both gained a `fieldClass` parameter (`community-livestream-mini-pill-purchase`/`-payment`, `community-livestream-status-select-purchase`/`-payment`) purely so `applyCommunityLivestreamStatusToCard(id, field, value)` can find the right pill/select inside a card that has two of each (not by DOM order/index). **Optimistic update with revert-on-failure**: the DOM is updated to the new value immediately (so the checkbox-style button/select feels instant), and if the `PATCH` fails, the exact same `applyCommunityLivestreamStatusToCard` call is made again with the pre-click value captured before the request started — this un-does the optimistic change rather than leaving the UI showing a state the database doesn't actually have, which is the specific failure mode the request called out. 商品名稱/備註 fields are untouched — still go through `patchCommunityLivestreamOrder`'s full-reload path, only 購買狀態/付款狀態 moved. **批次編輯模式 (功能三, prior round) does not touch either status field** — bulk-edit only ever stages `quantity`/`unitPrice`; both status controls keep saving immediately regardless of whether bulk-edit mode is on, per instructions ("批次編輯模式不用管這兩個狀態欄位").
- **New this round — card reflowed to fix mobile overflow, nickname dropped from display entirely**: the old `.community-livestream-card-header` packed "暱稱＋時間" (a `.community-livestream-customer-row`) stacked above a `<small>` LINE-display-name line, *plus* the 已購買/編輯/刪除 button trio, all in one `display:flex` row with no `flex-wrap` — on narrow (375px-class) viewports this reliably pushed the button trio outside the card's right edge. Fixed by: (1) **`order.nickname` is no longer rendered anywhere on this card** (it was the `社群暱稱` field, separate from `order.lineDisplayName` — see 功能一 above for why both columns exist; this round didn't remove the column or the Excel-export/search use of it, just stopped displaying it on the card itself); (2) the submitted-at timestamp moved from the header into `.community-livestream-thumb-wrap`, rendered as a new `.community-livestream-thumb-time` span directly below the photo/placeholder (plain block flow, no layout changes needed to the wrap itself since the img/placeholder are already block-level); (3) the header now holds exactly two things — a single-line `.community-livestream-customer-name` (LINE display name only, `flex:1;min-width:0` so a long name wraps/truncates instead of pushing the button out) and `.community-livestream-card-actions` holding just the 已購買 toggle (編輯/刪除 moved out, see next point) — `.community-livestream-card-header` itself gained `flex-wrap:wrap` as a backstop. (4) 編輯/刪除 relocated out of the header entirely, into a **new second line inside `.community-livestream-summary`** — `.community-livestream-summary-status-row` (`display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap`) holding a `.community-livestream-summary-pills` group (購買/匯款 mini-pills, left) and a second `.community-livestream-card-actions` instance (編輯/刪除, right — the same class is simply reused in two places now, its existing `margin-left:auto` does the right-alignment in both contexts without a new rule). (5) The 數量×單價=總價 line (`.community-livestream-summary-line`) is now its own row, no longer sharing space with the two status pills — those moved into the new status row described above. None of this touched `applyCommunityLivestreamStatusToCard`'s DOM lookups (prior round) since it targets everything by class, not by position, so the pill/select/button relocation didn't require touching that function at all. The "Photo enlarged + left/right split layout" bullet below is now partially stale where it describes the header as "nickname/time/LINE-name" — left as historical text (per this file's append-only-for-history convention within a still-accurate bullet) rather than rewritten, since the grid/photo-square mechanics it documents are otherwise still accurate; this new bullet is the current source of truth for the header/summary layout specifically.
- **Hard delete, no recovery, no audit trail (new this round)**: `DELETE /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/[id]` — same auth guard as `PATCH` (backend session + same-origin) — calls the new shared `deleteLivestreamOrder(supabase, orderId)` helper (`src/lib/line/livestream-orders.ts`, exported so both this route and 功能六's LINE self-cancel flow call the exact same delete, per instructions not to write it twice). Needs the new delete grant migration (`202609300003`, see above) — `service_role` previously only had `select/insert/update` on this table. The row's photo (if any) is **not** cleaned up from the private storage bucket on delete — left orphaned; not addressed this round, flagged here as a known gap rather than silently glossed over.
- **Batch select + batch status update**: each card has a checkbox overlaid on the top-left corner of its thumbnail (`.community-livestream-select-checkbox`, backed by a `communitySelectedLivestreamIds` Set that survives re-renders). A batch bar under the quick filters has **two independent** label+select+apply-button groups — one for 購買狀態, one for 匯款狀態 — deliberately kept as two separate controls rather than one shared "欄位/值" dropdown (per instructions, to avoid mis-clicks changing the wrong field). Applying either fires `PATCH .../livestream-orders/[id]` once per selected id via `Promise.all` (same pattern as 出貨申請's batch-link-save), then reloads the list and clears the selection. No new API route — reuses the existing single-item PATCH.
- **Batch delete (new this round)**: a red 「刪除已選項目」icon-only button (`fa-solid fa-trash`, `.btn-danger`) sits as a third item in the same batch bar, disabled under the same `communitySelectedLivestreamIds.size === 0` rule as the other two batch buttons (`updateCommunityLivestreamSelectionControls` now also toggles this button's `disabled`). Clicking it first runs a `confirm()` naming the exact count about to be deleted (irreversible, so this gets its own confirmation on top of the existing per-card delete's own `confirm()`) — declining does nothing. Confirming calls the **existing single-item** `DELETE /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/[id]` once per selected id via `Promise.all` (no new batch-delete API route, per instructions — same reuse pattern as the batch status buttons above, just `DELETE` instead of `PATCH`), then clears `communitySelectedLivestreamIds` and reloads the list — "已選 N 筆" and the 全選 checkbox reset the same way they already do after a batch status update, since both paths end in the identical clear-selection + reload sequence.
- **"全選" checkbox (new this round)**: sits next to the "共 N 筆・已選 N 筆" line (`.community-livestream-count-line` was changed from a plain text line to a flex row with `justify-content: space-between` to fit it). `toggleCommunityLivestreamSelectAll(checked)` adds/removes every id currently in `communityLivestreamOrders` (the already server-filtered/searched, client-loaded array — **not** a separate "all matching rows in the DB" query) to/from the exact same `communitySelectedLivestreamIds` Set the individual per-card checkboxes use, then calls the existing `renderCommunityLivestreamOrders()` to repaint every card's checkbox from that Set — no separate selection mechanism. Because it only ever touches ids already present in the loaded/filtered array, applying a filter/search first and then 全選 can never select a row that isn't currently visible. The checkbox's own state (checked/unchecked/**indeterminate**) is kept in sync by `updateCommunityLivestreamSelectAllState()`, called from the existing `updateCommunityLivestreamSelectionControls()` (already invoked after every render and after every individual checkbox toggle, so no new call sites were needed) — checked when every loaded row is selected, indeterminate when some but not all are, unchecked when none are.
- **Order time next to the buyer name + client-side sort by it (new this round)**: each card's top row now shows `storefrontDateTime(order.createdAt)` (same date-formatting helper every other page already uses, no new format invented) to the right of the nickname, in a small `.community-livestream-customer-row` flex row — no "下單時間：" label, just the timestamp itself, per spec. `created_at`/`createdAt` was already selected by the API and already used for the default server-side `order('created_at', { ascending: false })` — nothing changed there. A new "下單時間：最新在前／最舊在前" `<select>` sits next to 全選 in the count line; `setCommunityLivestreamSortOrder(value)` just sets a module-level `communityLivestreamSortOrder` ('desc' default) and re-renders. `sortCommunityLivestreamOrders()` (sorts the already-loaded `communityLivestreamOrders` array in place by `createdAt`, `asc`/`desc`) is called at the top of `renderCommunityLivestreamOrders()` itself — so it's purely client-side (no new query param, no API change) and automatically re-applies after every search/filter/reload/individual-field-edit re-render, since they all already funnel through that one render function.
- **Batch bar shortened + laid out side-by-side (new this round)**: labels shortened from "購買狀態改為"/"匯款狀態改為" to just "購買"/"匯款"; the "套用到已選項目" text buttons became icon-only (`fa-solid fa-check`, `aria-label` keeps them accessible); both groups now fit comfortably on one row at typical mobile widths (the shortened labels/icon buttons are what actually makes this fit — `.community-livestream-batch-bar` was already `display:flex` and wrapping only because the old, wordier groups didn't fit).
- **"共 N 筆" / "已選 N 筆" merged into one line, spacing tightened (new this round)**: both now live in the same `#community-livestream-result-count` element (as two inner `<span>`s — `#community-livestream-total-count-text` and the pre-existing `#community-livestream-selected-count`, each still updated independently by `renderCommunityLivestreamOrders`/`updateCommunityLivestreamSelectionControls`) rendered as `共 N 筆・已選 N 筆`, instead of two separately-positioned elements. Margins/gaps around the filter bar, batch bar, and card list were also tightened (`.community-livestream-filterbar`/`-quick-filters`/`-batch-bar`/`-list`/`-count-line`) to remove the large empty space the user flagged.
- **Toolbar icon-only on mobile (new this round)**: the section's own toolbar (收款帳號設定/匯入 Excel/匯出 Excel/重新整理) got a `.community-livestream-toolbar` marker class; its 4 button labels are wrapped in `<span class="btn-label">` and hidden only on ≤720px (`.community-livestream-toolbar .btn-label { display:none; }`, same "text stays in the DOM for screen readers, just visually hidden" technique as the earlier search-row icon change) — narrow enough now that all 4 fit on one row instead of wrapping to two. Desktop keeps full button text; this is scoped to this one page's toolbar only, not the shared `.toolbar` class.
- Search: a single keyword box matched client-side (JS `.includes()` after fetch) across LINE 名稱/社群暱稱/商品名 on the already-loaded (server status/unrecognized-filtered, capped at 500 rows) result set — no new search RPC. Was a deliberate choice over building a raw `.or()` ilike filter string from free-text input (easy to get the escaping wrong; this table has no dedicated search RPC the way `community_orders`/`community_shipment_requests` do).
- 6 one-click filter buttons (未購買/已購買/未付款/匯款確認中/已付款/無法辨識價格 — the last is `unit_price is null`), each independently toggleable (click again to clear), combine via `AND` when more than one is active; state lives in `communityLivestreamFilters` and round-trips through `GET .../livestream-orders?purchaseStatus=&paymentStatus=&unrecognizedOnly=1&q=`.
- **Customer price-dispute flagging + "價格異常" filter — added, then removed again (across two rounds)**: this used to work exactly like 無法辨識價格 above — a 7th quick-filter button plus a red `.community-livestream-dispute-info` card banner ("⚠ 客人回報：建議 NT$X") whenever `price_disputed_at` wasn't null, fed by a customer-facing "價格有誤" button in the (now also deleted) LINE quantity-confirmation screen. Once that customer-facing reporting flow was deleted (see 功能二 in the "LINE 圖片下單..." section above), the filter/banner could never be triggered by new data again, so the user confirmed removing them too rather than leaving permanently-dead UI — **both the quick-filter button and the card banner are gone**, along with the `priceDisputedOnly` filter state/query-param and its `toggleCommunityLivestreamQuickFilter` boolean-branch case. `price_disputed_at`/`price_dispute_suggested_price` (DB columns) and the `PATCH` route's auto-clear-on-price-edit logic are **left as-is**, same "don't touch schema/API just for a UI cleanup" convention as every other removed field in this feature — they simply never get set to a non-null value by anything anymore.
- Excel export: pure client-side (`XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet` + `XLSX.writeFile`, the same SheetJS CDN script `lily-backend.html` already loads) over whatever's currently loaded/filtered — no new API endpoint needed for this direction.
- Excel import ("手動新增訂單", not a LINE-bot backfill): same client-side `XLSX.read` + header-alias matching (`communityHeaderIndex`, reused as-is) pattern as the existing 記事本 import, landing in `POST /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/import` → `community_livestream_orders` with `source='excel_import'`, `line_user_id: null` (no real LINE identity for a manually-typed row, so no push notification will ever fire for these rows even after 功能四's trigger condition is met — that's expected, not a bug).
- **匯款確認中 cards show the customer's self-reported remittance**: when `paymentStatus === 'confirming'`, `GET .../livestream-orders` looks up the latest `community_livestream_remittances` row whose `order_ids` contains that order's id (one query per request, scoped to the `line_user_id`s actually present with `confirming` rows, matched in memory against the id array — not one query per card) and the card shows a small amber banner. **Now shows 銀行 too (this round)**: "客人回報：{銀行}／{金額}／末五碼{末五碼}" on one line (was "客人回報後 5 碼 / 申報金額" on two spans before) — see 功能五 below for why the customer now reports a bank name too. A row with `confirming` but no matching remittance (e.g. set manually via the batch tool) just shows no such line — nothing to display, not an error. **Bug fixed this round**: the API route previously did `Number(remittance.amount)` unconditionally — once `amount` became nullable (see the 拿掉客人端數量/價格互動 round above), a `null` amount silently became `0` (`Number(null) === 0`), so the card would have shown "NT$0" instead of "—" for every remittance with no recorded amount. Fixed to check `remittance.amount !== null` before converting; the frontend's own `=== null ? '—' : ...` check was already correct, it just never actually received `null`.
- **收款帳號設定 button — changed from one free-text field to three structured bank accounts (this round)**: the modal (`communityLivestreamBankInfoModal`) now has three separate inputs — 中信/富邦/國泰帳號 (fixed bank names/order, only the account number itself is editable) — via `GET`/`POST /api/backend/community/livestream-bank-info` → `src/lib/line/livestream-bank-info.ts::getLivestreamBankAccounts`/`saveLivestreamBankAccounts` → `schedule_settings` (`type='community-livestream-bank-info'`, JSON in `image`, same `type` key as before — a pre-existing free-text value just fails to parse as JSON and falls back to three blanks). Changed because 功能五's "我要匯款" message now sends three LINE Flex **clipboard** buttons instead of embedding account info in a text blob — each button needs its own distinct account string, which a single free-text field can't cleanly provide. A bank left blank simply has no corresponding button sent to customers (see 功能五 below).

### 功能四：訂單確認後的通知 — **removed** (this round)

- Originally: `PATCH /api/backend/community/livestream-orders/[id]` fired exactly one LINE **push** (`sendLineUserText`) the first time `purchase_status` transitioned to `'bought'` on a row with a `line_user_id`, guarded by `confirmed_notified_at` so later edits never re-sent it. **This has been removed** — the user decided the notification wasn't needed, since it was the only action in this entire feature that counted against the LINE OA's monthly message quota.
- Current behavior: the `PATCH` route now does a plain database update and nothing else — no `before`-row lookup, no push, no `notified` field in the response. Marking a row 已購買 (individually or via 功能三's batch tool) is silent from the customer's side.
- `confirmed_notified_at` (the column that used to guard against re-sending) is **left in the table as-is** — not read, not written, no migration to drop it. Removing an unused column wasn't worth a schema change for this; it's simply dead weight in `community_livestream_orders` now.
- This means the feature has **zero push messages anywhere** — see the note at the top of this section.

### 功能五：自助匯款申報

Brand-new LINE conversation flow, same trigger/reply pattern as "我要下單" (功能一/二). Only ever touches `community_livestream_orders.payment_status` and the new `community_livestream_remittances` table — does **not** touch `community_orders`/`community_remittance_submissions` (that older table stores the *customer's own* bank account the money came from, for the separate 記事本 system; this feature stores "which of *this* system's orders a report is meant to reconcile", a different concern entirely).

- Trigger: the `remittance` keyword group (default `我要匯款`/`匯款申報`/`回報匯款`/`匯款`, loose substring match, backend-configurable — see "Configurable trigger keywords" above) — no binding/approval gate (see 功能一 above), any LINE user can trigger this directly.
- On trigger, `handleRemittanceTrigger` queries that user's own `community_livestream_orders` where `payment_status = 'unpaid'` — no check for unrecognized prices (the old "block the whole batch if any row has `unit_price is null`" check was removed in an earlier round, since nothing calculates/displays money anymore). Every unpaid row is listed regardless of whether its price was ever recognized.
- **Photo list: shared "image + product name" style with 功能二's「傳好了」confirmation (settled across three rounds)**: the list was briefly pure-images-only (dropped the product-name text entirely), then reunified with 功能二's photo-confirm list into one shared enlarged-image style, then **this round reverted the image size back down to the old small-thumbnail look** — both flows call the exact same `buildPhotoNameItemBlock(row)` (see 功能二 above) instead of each maintaining its own item-block builder.
