Current shape: a 60px thumbnail on the left, product name as bold text on the right (`layout:"horizontal"`, matching the pre-removal `buildOrderItemBlock` sizing from back when this screen still had quantity buttons); a photo-less order simply omits the image, leaving just the name text (no grey placeholder box anymore — that was only needed for the brief enlarged-square-image version). The old `UnpaidOrderRow` type and `buildRemittanceItemBlock` function were deleted — this trigger now builds its rows as `PhotoNameOrderRow` and calls the shared builder directly. Joined into one bubble via the same shared `buildOrderListBubble` helper (separators between items, vertical stack — not a swipeable Carousel), unchanged from before.
- **Bank accounts delivered as three LINE Flex clipboard buttons, not embedded text (this round)**: `buildBankAccountButtonsBubble(accounts)` builds one button per non-blank account — label `複製{中信|富邦|國泰}帳號`, `action: { type: "clipboard", label, clipboardText: <該銀行帳號> }` — LINE's native clipboard action copies the text straight to the customer's clipboard with zero backend round-trip. Only banks the admin actually filled in get a button; if all three are blank, this whole message is skipped (not sent as an empty/button-less bubble). Backed by `src/lib/line/livestream-bank-info.ts`'s new structured `LivestreamBankAccounts` type (`{ zhongxin, fubon, cathay }`, fixed bank names/keys) — see 功能三 above for the admin-settings side of this change.
- **Reply is up to 3 messages**: `[Flex 商品清單 bubble] → [Flex 收款帳號按鈕 bubble, only if ≥1 bank is configured] → [文字：固定格式回報說明]` — well within LINE's 5-message reply cap.
- **State snapshot, order ids only**: at trigger time, `community_line_bot_states.awaiting_remittance_last5` is set `true` and the exact `order_ids` are written into that same table's `remittance_order_ids` column — a snapshot (so an order added/removed in the gap between listing and the customer's reply doesn't silently change what gets recorded). The `remittance_amount` bot-state column (used in an older version to snapshot a system-calculated total) is still not read/written — left in place, unused, same "don't drop it, just stop touching it" convention as every other removed field in this feature.
- **Customer self-calculates the total and reports a fixed 3-line format (this round, replacing the old "just reply 5 digits" flow)**: `remittance_summary`'s default text now asks the customer to total up the amount themselves and reply in the format `1.匯到哪家銀行：\n2.金額：\n3.末五碼：` — no `{{收款資訊}}` variable anymore (that information now arrives via the clipboard buttons above, not embedded in this text).
- **Lenient parsing, not exact-format matching**: `parseRemittanceReport(rawText)` (+ helper `extractReportFieldValue`) splits the reply into trimmed non-empty lines, then **independently** searches for a line containing each of 「銀行」/「金額」/「末五碼」(line order doesn't matter, numbering prefixes like "1."/"2." are ignored since they're not part of the keyword match, and the colon can be full-width "：" or half-width ":") and takes everything after the **first** colon on that line as the field's raw value. 銀行 just needs a non-empty value after the colon (capped at 50 chars). 金額 extracts the first run of digits found anywhere in its value after stripping commas (so "NT$500"/"500元"/"1,200" all parse to the right number) and requires it to be positive (greater than zero). 末五碼 strips every non-digit character from its value and requires **exactly** 5 digits left (so "12345", "1 2 3 4 5", and "末五碼：12345" all work, but a 6-digit string correctly fails rather than silently truncating). **All three must parse successfully** — if any one fails (missing, no colon, wrong format), the customer gets `remittance_format_invalid` (a format-reminder reply, same 3-line example) and the `awaiting_remittance_last5` state is **not** cleared, so they can just retry without re-triggering "我要匯款". Verified locally against shuffled-line-order, missing-numbering, and malformed inputs — all parse or correctly reject as designed (see commit for the test cases run).
- **Other known commands take priority over the "awaiting format" state + explicit `remittance_cancel` escape hatch (new this round)**: before this round, once `awaiting_remittance_last5` was set, *every* subsequent text message was fed straight into `parseRemittanceReport` — a customer who accidentally triggered "我要匯款" (or changed their mind) had no way out except guessing a valid-looking format or giving up. `handleTextMessage` now checks the new `remittance_cancel` keyword group (default `取消匯款`) **first, unconditionally, before even looking at the awaiting state** — if it matches while awaiting, it clears the state and replies `remittance_cancelled`; if it matches while *not* awaiting, it's a no-op (falls through to silence) rather than doing nothing special. Separately, while still in the awaiting state, if the text matches any of `order`/`remittance`/`cancel`/`photo_confirm` instead, the state is auto-cleared and the message falls through to that command's normal handler — so typing "我要下單"/"取消訂單"/"傳好了"/even "我要匯款" again all correctly escape the loop and do what they say, with the customer never perceiving they were "stuck". Only text matching none of these five groups is actually treated as a format submission. `remittance_format_invalid`'s default text now also points at the escape hatch ("如需取消匯款請輸入「取消匯款」。").
- **Real substring-matching bug found and fixed while building the escape hatch**: `matchesAny` does a plain `.includes()` check, and the chosen default keyword `取消匯款` literally contains both `取消` (the `cancel` group's own default keyword) and `匯款` (a substring of the `remittance` group's default `我要匯款`/`匯款`). Checking `remittance_cancel` only *inside* the `awaiting_remittance_last5` branch meant that outside that state, typing `取消匯款` would fall through to the generic dispatch and get matched by `keywords.remittance` (checked before `keywords.cancel`), silently **starting a brand-new remittance flow** — the exact opposite of this round's whole goal. Fixed by moving the `remittance_cancel` check to the very top of `handleTextMessage`, independent of and before the awaiting-state branch, with its own unconditional short-circuit (act on it only if awaiting, otherwise no-op either way) — confirmed via a local simulation script covering 11 scenarios (every known command from both inside and outside the awaiting state, the escape keyword from both states, a well-formed report, and garbage text) before shipping.
- **Real bug found and fixed this round — copy-pasting the bot's own prompt text got silently dropped**: 功能五's own `remittance_summary`/other prompt texts contain words like "完成"/"匯款" that are themselves default keywords (`photo_confirm`/`remittance`). Customers very commonly copy the entire prompt message, paste it back, then type their actual bank/amount/last-5 answers inline on the same lines — but the old `matchesKnownCommand` check ran **before** attempting to parse the format at all, so a pasted message containing "完成" or "匯款" anywhere in it got classified as "customer meant some other command" and the state was cleared **without ever calling `parseRemittanceReport`** — the customer's actual report was silently discarded, no success reply, no error reply, nothing recorded. Fixed by flipping the order: `handleTextMessage` now calls `parseRemittanceReport(text)` **first** while awaiting, and if it successfully parses a complete bank/amount/last-5 triple, proceeds straight to `handleRemittanceLast5Submission` regardless of what other keywords happen to also appear in the text — `matchesKnownCommand` is now only consulted when parsing actually fails. Verified with a throwaway `node -e` simulation of a realistic copy-pasted-prompt-plus-answers message (confirmed it both contains `remittance`/`photo_confirm` substrings **and** now correctly resolves to "submit", where the old logic resolved to "escape/discard") plus the same regression scenarios from the original escape-hatch round (plain known-command words still escape correctly when they *don't* also form a valid report, pure garbage still gets the format-invalid reminder).
- On a successful parse: inserts one row into `community_livestream_remittances` (`line_user_id`, `nickname`, `order_ids`, `bank_name`, `account_last5`, `amount` — the customer's **self-reported** amount, not a system calculation, purely for the admin to cross-check against the actual bank statement; needs the new `202610030001` migration's `bank_name` column, and relies on `202610020001`'s nullable `amount` column even though this flow now always supplies a value), updates all of those `order_ids`' `payment_status` to `confirming`, clears the bot state, and replies `remittance_success`. A DB failure on the insert leaves the awaiting state intact so the customer can just retry.
- `parseAccountLast5` (the old "the entire message is just 5 digits" parser) was deleted — fully superseded by `parseRemittanceReport`.
- Admin side: the reported bank/amount/last-5 show on the order's card once `payment_status='confirming'` (功能三 above); after the admin reconciles against the actual bank statement, they use 功能三's batch tool to flip the reconciled cards' 匯款狀態 to 已付款 in one action.
- Entirely reply-based like 功能一/二 — no push (功能四's push was removed entirely in an earlier round; this flow was never affected by it either way since it only ever touched `payment_status`, not `purchase_status`).

### 功能六：自助取消訂單

Brand-new LINE conversation flow, same trigger/reply pattern as "我要下單"/"我要匯款". Only ever touches `community_livestream_orders` — a customer can only cancel their own **not-yet-purchased AND not-yet-in-payment** items, and cancelling means the row is gone (hard delete, no cancellation log/audit trail — explicitly deferred, not built this round).

- Trigger: the `cancel` keyword group (default `取消訂單`/`我要取消`/`取消`, loose substring match, backend-configurable — see "Configurable trigger keywords" above) — **no binding/approval gate anymore** (see 功能一 above), any LINE user can trigger this directly. **`查詢訂單` briefly lived inside this group as an alias in the prior round — that was wrong and has been corrected**: this round pulled it back out into its own independent `order_query` group/feature (see 功能七 below), since "query everything I've ordered" and "show me what I can still cancel" turned out to need different underlying queries, not just two names for the same list.
- `handleCancelTrigger` queries that user's own `community_livestream_orders` where `purchase_status = 'not_bought'` **and** `payment_status = 'unpaid'` (oldest first, capped at 50 like the other list-building flows) — **both conditions changed this round**: originally this only checked `purchase_status`, which meant a customer who had already self-reported a remittance (功能五, `payment_status` flipped to `confirming`) could still cancel an order the admin was actively reconciling against a bank statement. Now `payment_status` must also still be `unpaid`.
  - None found → replies `目前沒有可以取消的商品喔（已購買或已在匯款流程中的商品無法取消）。` and stops.
  - Otherwise builds a **vertical list, not a Carousel (layout changed this round)** — same batching the cancel flow always used (groups of 10, up to 4 batches since one slot is used by the leading text message, so up to `MAX_REPLY_MESSAGES - 1` batches), but each batch is now one Flex bubble built via the existing `buildOrderListBubble` (separator-divided vertical stack, the same container 功能五/七 already use), not a `{ type: "carousel", contents: [...] }` of individually swipeable bubbles. Each row is a **3-column horizontal block** via the new `buildCancelItemBlock` (replacing the deleted `buildCancelBubble`): 60px thumbnail on the left, product name in the middle (`flex:1`, same text styling as `buildPhotoNameItemBlock`), and the red "取消這項" postback button (`action=cancel_order&order_id=...`) on the right — all three in one row, not stacked (photo-on-top/button-below, the old per-bubble layout). `CancellableOrderRow`'s shape is unchanged (`id`/`product_name`/`photo_storage_path`, still no `quantity`). A leading text message is prepended, covering both non-cancellable reasons in one count: if the customer has any rows that are either `bought` or already past `unpaid`, it says `您已購買或已在匯款流程中的 N 項商品不會列在這裡，恕無法取消。以下是可以取消的商品：` (`N` = this user's total row count minus the cancellable count — cheaper than a second OR-condition query, and exactly equivalent since both queries share the same `line_user_id` scope); otherwise just `以下是您目前可以取消的商品：`.
- Tapping "取消這項" → `handlePostback`'s `cancel_order` branch → `handleCancelOrder`: re-reads that row scoped by **both** `id` and `line_user_id` (so a customer can't cancel someone else's order id even by guessing/replaying an old postback), re-checks `purchase_status === 'not_bought'` (defends against a race where the admin bought it in the meantime — replies `這項商品已經購買，無法取消。` if so), then re-checks `payment_status === 'unpaid'` (defends against a race where the customer submitted a remittance report in the gap between opening the cancel carousel and tapping the button — replies `這項商品已在匯款流程中，無法取消。`, deliberately different wording from the purchased-block message so the customer knows which reason applied), then calls the same `deleteLivestreamOrder` the backend's delete button uses, and replies `已為您取消「商品名稱」，感謝您的訂購！`. **This flow never had a `quantity_locked_at` check** (that concept only ever applied to 功能二's now-deleted quantity-confirmation screen) — nothing to remove here even though "拿掉鎖定限制" was requested defensively this round; `purchase_status`/`payment_status` remain the only two gating conditions, unchanged.
- No push anywhere in this flow — same reply-only design as the rest of the feature.

### 功能七：查詢訂單 (**new this round**, split out of 功能六's mistaken `cancel`-alias)

Brand-new, fully independent LINE conversation flow — not a view mode of 功能六's cancel list. Only ever reads `community_livestream_orders`, never writes anything.

- Trigger: the new `order_query` keyword group (default just `查詢訂單`, loose substring match, backend-configurable — see "Configurable trigger keywords" above) — no binding/approval gate, any LINE user can trigger this directly. Checked in `handleTextMessage` right after `cancel` and before `photo_confirm` (dispatch order: order → remittance → cancel → order_query → photo_confirm, after the always-first `remittance_cancel` check) — also added to the `awaiting_remittance_last5` state's "other known commands take priority" list (功能五 above) alongside the other four groups, so a customer stuck waiting for a remittance format who types "查詢訂單" correctly escapes that state too.
- `handleOrderQueryTrigger` queries that user's own `community_livestream_orders` with **no `purchase_status`/`payment_status` filter at all** (capped at 50 like the other list-building flows, oldest first) — deliberately the opposite of 功能六's cancel list: already-`bought`/already-`paid`/`confirming` rows are all included, since this is meant to answer "what have I ordered, full stop," not "what can I still touch."
- No rows at all → replies `order_query_empty` (default "目前沒有查到您的下單紀錄喔。") and stops.
- Otherwise: one short intro text (`order_query_intro`, default "以下是您目前下單的所有商品：") followed by one or more Flex bubbles (same `MAX_PHOTOS_PER_ROUND`-per-batch / `MAX_REPLY_MESSAGES - 1`-batches chunking as 功能六) built with the **exact same `buildPhotoNameItemBlock` + `buildOrderListBubble`** 功能五's "我要匯款" list already uses — 60px thumbnail left, product name right, vertical separator-divided stack, **no buttons, no quantity, no price** — intentionally identical styling to 匯款's list, per instructions ("樣式比照我要匯款").
- No push anywhere in this flow — same reply-only design as the rest of the feature.

### 功能八：後台「匯款紀錄」頁籤 — 這輪從扁平卡片列表改成「人名 → 匯款紀錄」雙層結構

Backend tab for managing `community_livestream_remittances` directly — until it existed at all, this table had **no admin UI of its own**, only a read-only "客人回報：{銀行}/{金額}/末五碼{末五碼}" snippet surfaced on a `confirming`-status order card in 功能三's 社群連線訂單 tab. The flat-card-list version of this tab (前一輪) has been superseded this round by a nested "每個客人一個區塊，底下直接列出他全部的匯款紀錄＋未匯款商品" layout — see below for the new shape; the underlying table/migration/basic CRUD semantics are unchanged.

- **銀行名稱正規化（this round, 完全新的部分）**：`src/lib/line/livestream-bank-aliases.ts`（新檔案）新增 `normalizeLivestreamBankName(raw)`（別名表，目前只有「中國信託」→「中信」，每個標準名稱也各自列一條「自己對應自己」，之後要擴充別名很容易加一行）跟 `isLivestreamBankNameAnomaly(bankName)`（查無此名／null 都算異常）。標準名稱清單直接 `Object.values(LIVESTREAM_BANK_ACCOUNT_LABELS)`（中信/富邦/國泰），不另開一份容易跟功能三的收款帳號設定對不起來的清單。`src/lib/line/livestream-orders.ts::parseRemittanceReport` 解析出銀行名稱後立刻呼叫這支正規化——對照不到的名稱**原文照存，不會擋下整筆匯款申報**，單純在後台被標成「異常」，等管理員用下拉選單重新指定成正確的那個。**既有舊資料不做任何轉換/清理**，純粹是這輪新寫入的資料才會被正規化。
- **後台銀行欄位從自由輸入框改成下拉選單（this round）**：`PATCH /api/backend/community/livestream-remittances/[id]` 的 `bankName` 驗證收緊——只接受 `LIVESTREAM_STANDARD_BANK_NAMES`（中信/富邦/國泰）三個值，不是任意字串了，不符合就回 400「請選擇正確的銀行。」。前端的 `<select>`（`communityRemittanceBankSelectHtml`）本身只有這三個標準選項，但如果這筆紀錄目前的值是「異常」，會額外插一個選中、帶 ⚠ 圖示的選項顯示實際值——不然 `<select>` 會因為找不到對應 option 而默默選到清單第一個標準銀行，誤導管理員以為資料其實正常。
- **`GET /api/backend/community/livestream-remittances` 整個改成依客人分組的巢狀結構（this round，取代原本的扁平陣列）**：回傳 `{ customers: [{ lineUserId, lineDisplayName, remittances: [...], unpaid: {...} | null }] }`。客人清單＝`community_livestream_orders` 跟 `community_livestream_remittances` 兩張表 `line_user_id` 的聯集（在記憶體裡用兩個 `Map` 分組算出來，不是 SQL join）——只下過單、一次都沒匯款申報過的人也會出現，只是 `remittances` 是空陣列、只有 `unpaid` 那一行。每筆 `remittances[]` 項目帶 `bankName`/`isBankAnomaly`/`accountLast5`/`amount`（客人回報）/`systemCalculatedTotal`（查 `order_ids` 對應訂單重新算出來的 `Σ quantity×unitPrice`，`unitPrice` 是 null 的訂單當 0 計入）/`mismatch`（`amount !== null && amount !== systemCalculatedTotal`）/`submittedAt`/`reviewedAt`/`orders`（該筆涵蓋的訂單明細，`{id, productName, photoUrl, quantity, unitPrice, lineTotal}`，`lineTotal` 是 null 代表這筆單價未辨識，跟聚合用的 `systemCalculatedTotal` 的「null 當 0」處理故意不同——一個是給人看的單筆明細，一個是給聚合加總用的）。`unpaid` 只在這個人目前有 `payment_status='unpaid'` 的訂單時才不是 `null`，形狀跟 `remittances[]` 項目類似但沒有銀行/末五碼/核對狀態那些欄位。簽名 URL 只對實際用得到的訂單照片簽（匯款紀錄關聯到的 + 目前未付款的），不是對這個客人全部訂單都簽一次。
- **排序規則（this round，目前這樣實作，不是使用者明確講過的規則，之後要改隨時可以改）**：先依「這個人最新一筆匯款」對應的標準銀行名稱排序（`LIVESTREAM_BANK_ACCOUNT_KEYS` 的順序：中信→富邦→國泰→異常排最後），同銀行內再依匯款時間新到舊排序；完全沒有任何匯款紀錄的人排在整個列表最後，這些人之間依 `lineDisplayName`（`localeCompare` with `'zh-Hant'`）排序。用一支 node -e 腳本模擬過這個排序邏輯（6 個假客人，涵蓋中信/富邦/異常/無匯款四種情況），順序符合預期。
- **銀行篩選取代原本的未核對/已核對快篩（this round）**：query 參數改成 `bank`（`zhongxin`/`fubon`/`cathay`/`anomaly`/空字串＝全部，跟收款帳號設定用同一套 key，不是直接傳中文）。篩選邏輯是「這個人名下只要有任一筆匯款符合選中的銀行」整組客人就顯示出來，不是只看其中一筆——`customers.filter(c => c.remittances.some(r => ...))`。`reviewed` 篩選參數整個拿掉，被這個取代；原本的 `q` 搜尋客人名稱保留，邏輯不變（只比對 `lineDisplayName`，這張表的名稱欄位本來就只有一個，見上一輪的說明）。
- **後台畫面重新設計成雙層結構（this round）**：第一層「人名」永遠展開顯示（不是手風琴，跟功能三「依客人統計」那套手風琴刻意不一樣，因為這裡每個人底下的東西本來就不多，沒有收合的必要，使用者原話是「每個人底下的東西永遠展開顯示」）；第二層是這個人的每一筆匯款紀錄，由上往下直接列出（`.community-remittances-row`），每一列顯示：展開/收合按鈕、銀行下拉選單、末五碼輸入框、時間、已核對/未核對小圓點、系統計算總額、客人回報金額輸入框（兩者不同就顯示 ⚠，`.community-remittances-mismatch-badge`）、標記已核對/取消標記按鈕、刪除按鈕（`confirm()` 確認）。點展開按鈕會秀出這筆匯款涵蓋的商品明細（縮圖+名稱+數量×單價+小計，跟社群連線訂單卡片縮圖風格一致但尺寸更小）。固定排在每個人紀錄最後一行的「未匯款」也是同一套 `.community-remittances-row` 外觀（只是少了銀行/末五碼/核對狀態那些欄位，背景色不同做區分），這個人目前沒有任何未付款訂單就完全不顯示這一行。編輯/刪除/標記已核對**沿用原本的單筆 `PATCH`/`DELETE` 端點**，只是前端呼叫端要能處理巢狀資料——存檔後整份重新載入（不是局部 DOM 更新，這張表操作頻率不像社群連線訂單的單價/數量那麼高，沒必要為了這次的巢狀結構另外做一套局部更新機制）。兩層的展開狀態（哪些匯款紀錄/哪些人的未匯款行展開著）各自用一個 module 層級的 `Set` 記著，不靠 DOM 反查。
- **後台手動新增匯款紀錄（this round）**：新增 `POST /api/backend/community/livestream-remittances`（同一個檔案，跟既有的 `GET` 共用 `guardBackendRequest`，補上 `mutation` 參數跟 `isSameOriginMutation` 檢查，比照 `livestream-keywords`/`livestream-reply-templates` 路由的既有寫法）。給客人一直沒辦法透過 LINE 自助送出匯款格式的情況直接在後台補登一筆：驗證 `lineUserId` 非空、`orderIds` 是非空字串陣列、`bankName` 必須是三個標準名稱之一、`accountLast5` 符合 5 碼數字、`amount` 可為 `null` 或非負數字。**寫入前用「符合條件的筆數剛好等於請求的筆數」確認每一筆 `orderId` 都真的屬於這個 `lineUserId` 且目前還是 `unpaid`**（一次 `select...in()` 查詢，不是逐筆檢查），避免前端資料過期或被竄改選到不相干/已處理過的訂單，任何一筆不符合就整個 409 擋下，不會部分寫入。`nickname` 存這個客人目前的 `line_display_name` 快照（跟 LINE 那條路徑的欄位語意一致）；寫入 `community_livestream_remittances` 後，同一批 `orderIds` 的 `payment_status` 也改成 `confirming`（比照 `handleRemittanceLast5Submission` 最後那段 update，不然這筆手動補登的紀錄會跟訂單實際狀態對不起來，那幾筆訂單在別的地方還是顯示「未匯款」）。前端「新增匯款紀錄」按鈕開一個 Modal：選客人（下拉選單，只列 `unpaid` 不是 `null` 的人，直接濾 `communityLivestreamRemittanceCustomers`，不開新查詢端點）→ 列出該客人的未付款訂單（每筆一個 checkbox，預設**不勾**，上面一顆「全選」按鈕一次勾滿、勾完還是能再取消個別項目）→ 填銀行/末五碼/金額。成功後整份重新載入（不是高頻操作，沒做局部更新）。
- **匯款紀錄匯出 Excel（this round）**：工具列新增「匯出 Excel」，直接攤平已經載入的 `communityLivestreamRemittanceCustomers`（不額外打 API）成一筆匯款紀錄一列——客人名稱/銀行/末五碼/客人回報金額/系統計算金額/是否核對/匯款時間，**不含**每筆的商品明細、也**不含**「未匯款」那一行（那不是一筆已申報的紀錄）。沿用社群連線訂單匯出已經在用的同一個 `XLSX` 全域（頁面本來就載入過，沒有另外載入新的函式庫）。
- Not yet manually tested (needs the pending migrations, same blocker as everything else in this feature) — see the updated manual test list below.

### 功能九：社群連線訂單「依客人統計」檢視 (**new this round**)

New view mode inside 功能三的社群連線訂單 tab, deliberately separate from 功能八的匯款紀錄 tab even though both show "per-customer totals" — this one is about helping the admin eyeball whether a given customer's order total vs. their reconciled/pending remittances line up, scoped to orders already loaded in this tab; 功能八 is the actual record-management surface for the remittance rows themselves.

- **`GET /api/backend/community/livestream-orders` gained a `lineUserId` field**: previously the only identity-ish fields returned were `lineDisplayName`/`nickname` (display strings, not a real stable id) — the frontend had no reliable way to group rows by actual customer identity without string-matching display names, which isn't safe (two different LINE accounts could theoretically share a display name). Now returns the real `line_user_id` directly.
- **New `GET /api/backend/community/livestream-customer-summary`**: same auth-guard/rate-limit shape as every other route here. Groups `community_livestream_orders` + `community_livestream_remittances` by `line_user_id` in memory (two queries, capped at 5000 rows each — no real pagination, same "big enough cap, not true pagination" convention as the new 功能八 nested-GET route) and returns, per customer: `totalAmount` (`Σ quantity×unitPrice` across **all** of that customer's orders regardless of status, `unitPrice` null → 0, no flag), `confirmedRemittanceAmount` (`Σ amount` across remittances where `reviewed_at` is not null, null `amount` → 0), `pendingRemittanceAmount` (same but `reviewed_at is null`), and `diffAmount = totalAmount - confirmedRemittanceAmount`. `lineDisplayName` comes from that customer's most recent order (orders are already queried newest-first, so the first row encountered per `line_user_id` is it).
- **Toolbar toggle "依客人統計"** (`#community-livestream-customer-view-toggle-btn`, same swap-`btn-secondary`/`btn-primary` + relabel pattern as the existing 批次編輯 toggle). Turning it on fetches the summary endpoint once and switches `renderCommunityLivestreamOrders()` to a grouped-accordion layout instead of the flat time-sorted list; turning it off switches back, no refetch needed (the underlying `communityLivestreamOrders` array never changes shape, only how it's grouped for display).
- **Card template extracted into `buildCommunityLivestreamOrderCardHtml(order, openIds)`** so the flat list and the new per-customer accordion render *the exact same card markup* — one function, two call sites. This was a pure refactor of the existing render function's inner `.map()` callback into its own named function; no visual/behavioral change to the flat view. Quantity/unit-price/status edits inside an expanded accordion group call the exact same `patchCommunityLivestreamQuantity`/`patchCommunityLivestreamUnitPrice`/`patchCommunityLivestreamStatus`/`stageCommunityLivestreamFieldChange` functions as the flat view — those functions were **not** touched, per instructions, and didn't need to be: they already operate purely by order id, with no assumption about where in the DOM the card lives.
- **Each customer group**: collapsed by default (`communityLivestreamExpandedCustomerIds` Set tracks which are open, survives re-renders since it's the actual state source, not scraped from the DOM), header shows the customer's `lineDisplayName` + all 4 summary amounts, sorted by `lineDisplayName` (`localeCompare` with `'zh-Hant'`). Expanding shows that customer's order cards (grouped from the already-loaded, already-filtered/sorted `communityLivestreamOrders` array — grouping happens purely client-side, no extra API call per expand).
- **Known staleness, accepted deliberately**: the 4 header amounts only refresh when the summary endpoint is re-fetched — which happens on toggling the view on, and (hooked into `loadCommunityLivestreamOrders()`) on any action that already does a full reload (重新整理, search/filter, product-name/notes edits, delete). Quantity/unit-price/status edits use their existing **local-DOM-only** update path (no full reload, by design, from earlier rounds) and do **not** refresh the header amounts — per instructions not to touch those three functions, this is accepted staleness, not a bug: the numbers reflect "as of last full reload," and an admin who just changed a price would need to reload (or re-toggle the view) to see the header catch up. Worth revisiting in a future round if this proves confusing in practice.
- Not yet manually tested (needs the pending migrations, same blocker as everything else in this feature) — see the updated manual test list below.

### New library files (all `server-only`)

- `src/lib/line/webhook-security.ts` — `verifyLineWebhookSignature(rawBody, signatureHeader)` (HMAC-SHA256 with `LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET`, `timingSafeEqual` comparison) + `isLineWebhookConfigured()`.
- `src/lib/line/vision.ts` — `recognizeProductPhoto(buffer, contentType)`, plain `fetch` to `https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` (model `claude-sonnet-5`, vision content block), no SDK dependency added (matches this codebase's existing "plain fetch, no provider SDKs" convention for `src/lib/line/client.ts`). Returns `{ productName, price, confidence }` with any/all fields `null` if the model can't determine them — never guesses. Returns `null` entirely only if it couldn't run at all (no API key / network / provider error).
- `src/lib/supabase/storage.ts` — `uploadPrivateFile(bucket, path, buffer, contentType)` / `createSignedUrl(bucket, path, expiresInSeconds)`. First use of Supabase Storage anywhere in this codebase (everything else uses Cloudinary, which is a **public**-URL service — wrong fit for private customer photos, so this feature uses Supabase Storage instead, not Cloudinary).
- `src/lib/line/livestream-orders.ts` — all the conversation logic for 功能一/二/五/六/七 (`handleLineEvent` is the single entry point the webhook route calls per incoming event). Also exports `deleteLivestreamOrder(supabase, orderId)` and the `SupabaseService` type, shared with the backend's `DELETE` route. **This round**: `parseRemittanceReport`'s bank-name parsing now runs the result through `normalizeLivestreamBankName` (see `livestream-bank-aliases.ts` below) before storing it — the only change to this file this round, the rest of the conversation logic is untouched.
- `src/lib/line/livestream-bank-info.ts` — `getLivestreamBankAccounts()`/`saveLivestreamBankAccounts(next)`, backing 功能三's 收款帳號設定 button and 功能五's clipboard buttons. **Changed from one free-text blob to a structured 3-account object this round** (`{ zhongxin, fubon, cathay }`, fixed bank names/keys, each account an independently-editable string) — see 功能五 below for why. `schedule_settings` KV, same pattern as `community-notification-templates.ts`; reuses the same `type` key as the old free-text version, so any pre-existing free-text value just fails `JSON.parse` and falls back to three blank accounts (acceptable — this feature has no real data yet, migrations still pending).
- `src/app/api/backend/community/livestream-bank-info/route.ts` — `GET`/`POST` for the above, same auth-guard shape as every other backend route in this feature.
- `src/lib/line/livestream-keywords.ts` — `getLivestreamKeywords()`/`saveLivestreamKeywords(next)`/`findDuplicateKeywords(groups)`, backing the "Configurable trigger keywords" section above. Same `schedule_settings` KV pattern, falls back to `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_KEYWORDS` on any read failure.
- `src/app/api/backend/community/livestream-keywords/route.ts` — `GET`/`POST` for the above; `POST` returns `needsConfirm` (not a hard error) when it finds the same keyword in more than one group.
- `src/lib/line/livestream-reply-templates.ts` — **new this round**: `getLivestreamReplyTemplates()`/`saveLivestreamReplyTemplate(key, text)`/`renderLivestreamReplyTemplate(template, vars)`, backing the "Configurable reply templates" section above. Same `schedule_settings` KV pattern, falls back to `DEFAULT_LIVESTREAM_REPLY_TEMPLATES` on any read failure or blank per-key value.
- `src/app/api/backend/community/livestream-reply-templates/route.ts` — `GET`/`POST` for the above.
- `src/app/api/backend/community/livestream-remittances/route.ts` / `.../[id]/route.ts` — `GET` (customer-nested list, `q`/`bank` filters), `POST` (**new this round**, manual backend-side entry, see 功能八 above), `PATCH` (edit bank name/last-5/amount, toggle `reviewed_at` — `bankName` now validated against the 3 standard names), `DELETE` (hard delete) for `community_livestream_remittances`. `route.ts`'s shared `guardBackendRequest` gained the same `mutation` boolean parameter the other dual GET+POST routes in this feature already use (`livestream-keywords`/`livestream-reply-templates`) — both still require a valid backend session, but only `POST` additionally enforces `isSameOriginMutation` (the same-origin/CSRF check); `GET` doesn't need it since it's read-only. No shared lib file for the query logic itself; lives directly in the route handler, same as 功能三's `livestream-orders` list/PATCH/DELETE routes.
- `src/lib/line/livestream-bank-aliases.ts` — **new this round**: `normalizeLivestreamBankName(raw)` (alias map → standard name, currently just 中國信託→中信, each standard name also maps to itself), `isLivestreamBankNameAnomaly(bankName)`, `LIVESTREAM_STANDARD_BANK_NAMES` (= `Object.values(LIVESTREAM_BANK_ACCOUNT_LABELS)`, so it can never drift from the 3-bank list `livestream-bank-info.ts` already defines). Used by `parseRemittanceReport` (livestream-orders.ts, normalizes what the customer typed), the remittances `PATCH` route (validates the dropdown's value), and the remittances `GET` route (computes `isBankAnomaly` per row and implements the `anomaly` bank-filter value) — backing 功能八 above.
- `src/app/api/backend/community/livestream-customer-summary/route.ts` — **new this round**: `GET` only, no mutation — per-`line_user_id` aggregated totals (`totalAmount`/`confirmedRemittanceAmount`/`pendingRemittanceAmount`/`diffAmount`) backing 功能九's "依客人統計" accordion header. No shared lib file, same "query lives in the route" convention as this feature's other list routes.
- `src/lib/line/client.ts` — extended (not replaced) with `sendLineReply`/`sendLineReplyText`/`sendLineReplyFlex` (reply, never throws — logs+returns `disabled:true` on any failure so a webhook handler always resolves cleanly), `getLineUserProfile`, `downloadLineMessageContent`. All existing push functions (`sendLineAdminText`/`sendLineUserText`/etc.) are untouched.
- `src/app/api/line/webhook/route.ts` — reads the raw body with `request.text()` (required for signature verification — never `.json()` first), verifies, parses, dispatches every event in the array through `handleLineEvent` in parallel, always resolves 200 regardless of per-event outcome (LINE retries aggressively on non-2xx).
- `src/lib/line/price-ocr.ts` — **new this round**: `recognizePriceFromPhoto(buffer)`, the free white-box OCR fallback described in 功能二 above. New runtime deps: `sharp@0.34.5` (pixel scan/crop — pinned to match Next.js's own bundled version, see "Deployment notes" below), `tesseract.js@7.0.0` (OCR). No new API route — only called from inside `handleImageMessage`.

### Deployment notes: `sharp` and `tesseract.js` on Vercel

Real, deployment-breaking issues surfaced getting `price-ocr.ts` running on Vercel — worth remembering for any future dependency that touches native/WASM modules or `worker_threads`:

- **`sharp().greyscale()` silently breaks `tesseract.js` OCR in this stack.** The crop pipeline originally converted to greyscale before OCR "for cleaner contrast" — this made `tesseract.js` return empty text (0 confidence) on every image, no error thrown. Root cause: `.greyscale()` makes `sharp` output a single-channel PNG, which this version of `tesseract.js`/Leptonica fails to read correctly. Fix: dropped `.greyscale()` entirely — `.normalise()` alone gives plenty of contrast for a white-background/black-text price sticker and doesn't have this problem. Caught by building a synthetic local sanity test (see "Verification" below) rather than by the real reference photos, which weren't accessible in this environment.
- **Two different `sharp` versions in the tree crashed the whole webhook function on Vercel.** Next.js 16.1.6 already depends on `sharp@0.34.5` internally (for `next/image`). Installing our own `sharp@^0.35.5` left both versions installed side by side (`npm ls sharp --all` showed `next@16.1.6 > sharp@0.34.5` **and** a separate top-level `sharp@0.35.5`). On Vercel's Node runtime this produced `ERR_DLOPEN_FAILED: libvips-cpp.so.8.18.7: cannot open shared object file`, which crashed `/api/line/webhook` with a `500` on **every** request (not just image messages) — confirmed via `vercel logs`, and confirmed it wasn't a stale-build-cache artifact by forcing a genuinely fresh, uncached install (`vercel deploy --force`) and seeing the identical error. Also tried marking `sharp` as a Next.js `serverExternalPackages` entry (`next.config.ts`) first, since that's the commonly-cited fix for this exact error message online — it changed how the module was bundled (confirmed via the error message switching to reference an "external module" wrapper) but did **not** fix the crash, so that theory was wrong for this specific case; left the config change in anyway since it's still correct general guidance for native modules and doesn't hurt. The fix that actually worked: pinned our own `sharp` to the exact `0.34.5` Next.js already ships and uses successfully in production, which dedupes to a single copy — confirmed via `npm ls sharp --all` showing `sharp@0.34.5 deduped`, and confirmed on Vercel by `curl -X POST .../api/line/webhook` returning `401` (the expected "route alive, signature check working" response) instead of `500`.
- Practical takeaway: if any future dependency added to this repo also wants `sharp` (or another native/optional-binary package Next.js itself bundles), check `npm ls <package> --all` for version conflicts *before* assuming a clean Vercel deploy — a conflict here doesn't show up as a build failure, only as a runtime crash on whichever route happens to load the module.
- **`tesseract.js`'s worker thread crashed Production outright — a separate, later incident, full writeup in "Production incident" above.** Short version: `new Worker(path.join(__dirname, ...))` inside tesseract.js's own source computes a path that Turbopack's `__dirname` rewriting breaks post-deploy; `outputFileTracingIncludes` alone (despite confirmably including the right file bytes) did **not** fix it, since the problem was never about missing files. The fix needed `serverExternalPackages: ["tesseract.js", "tesseract.js-core"]` **plus** an explicit `workerPath` passed to `createWorker()` — matching the two independently-reported fixes on [naptha/tesseract.js#868](https://github.com/naptha/tesseract.js/issues/868). Practical takeaway: any package that spawns a `worker_threads.Worker` with a dynamically-computed entry path (not a literal `require()`/`import`) is at risk of this exact class of bug under Turbopack — check upstream issue trackers for "Next.js"/"Vercel"/"worker" before assuming a library "just works" in this environment, and don't trust a clean local build as proof — this bug reproduced 100% of the time on every real Vercel deploy while never once reproducing locally.
- Unrelated to the above but hit along the way: local `vercel deploy` failed outright with `EPERM: operation not permitted, lstat '...\.tmp-community-psycopg\psycopg'` — a stray, unrelated, permission-locked directory (looked like a Python `psycopg`/`tzdata` install, already listed in `.vercelignore` on disk but Vercel's local file walk still choked on it) sitting in the project root. Not created by this round's work; relocated it out of the project tree (`C:\Users\lily.deng\Desktop\_quarantined-tmp-community-psycopg`, not deleted — its own subfolders were themselves unreadable/undeletable, likely broken reparse points) with explicit user confirmation before touching it.

### Local verification of `price-ocr.ts` without LINE/webhook (new this round)

`recognizePriceFromPhoto(buffer)` can be exercised directly against real image files on disk — no LINE account, no webhook, no migrations needed, since it's a pure function that only touches `sharp`/`tesseract.js`. This is how the real reference photos got tested once they were finally available as files (`scratch/` is gitignored/vercelignored, safe to drop real photos into without them ever being committed or deployed):

- Drop sample images into `scratch/price-ocr-samples/` and reuse `scratch/test-price-ocr.ts` (reads every image in that folder, calls `recognizePriceFromPhoto`, prints filename/result/elapsed-ms) — kept in place for anyone to rerun the same way.
- This project has no `tsx`/`ts-node` installed, and `import "server-only"` (a Next.js build-time-only marker package that isn't a real npm package in `node_modules`) makes a plain `ts-node`/`tsx` run fail outside Next's own bundler. The workaround that works in this environment: (1) create a throwaway no-op shim at `node_modules/server-only/{package.json,index.js}` (`module.exports = {}`) so plain Node can resolve the import — delete it again afterward, never commit it; (2) transpile with the project's own `tsc` directly to plain CommonJS, preserving the relative folder structure so the script's own relative import keeps working: `npx tsc --module commonjs --target es2020 --esModuleInterop --skipLibCheck --ignoreConfig --rootDir . --outDir scripts/_tmp-ts-out scratch/test-price-ocr.ts src/lib/line/price-ocr.ts` (run via the PowerShell tool, not Bash — same long-standing environment quirk as every other `npx`/`tsc` call in this project); (3) copy `scratch/price-ocr-samples/` into the matching path under `scripts/_tmp-ts-out/scratch/` (the compiled script's `__dirname` resolves there, not back to the real `scratch/`); (4) `node scripts/_tmp-ts-out/scratch/test-price-ocr.js`.
- **The script will hang after printing every result and never exit on its own** — this is expected, not a bug: `recognizePriceFromPhoto` deliberately keeps its shared `tesseract.js` worker (a `worker_thread`) alive for reuse across warm serverless invocations in production, and a plain one-shot Node script has nothing else keeping the event loop busy once the results are printed, so the live worker thread is the only thing left holding the process open. Just stop the process once the output looks complete (or add a temporary `process.exit(0)` at the end of a copy of the script) — don't take this as a sign anything is broken.
- Delete `scripts/_tmp-ts-out/` and the `node_modules/server-only` shim afterward; `scratch/` itself needs no cleanup (already gitignored/vercelignored).

### Explicitly out of scope this round (per instructions)

- No changes to `community_orders`/`community_order_items`/the 記事本 admin UI/API.
- No changes to the existing binding-review page or its API (`社群名單` tab, `/api/backend/community/members`) — 功能一 originally wrote the same columns that page reads, but this round removed 功能一's binding/review mechanism entirely (see 功能一 above), so this feature now doesn't touch `community_line_bindings` at all; that page/API is for the separate old 記事本/社群訂單 system.
- No "ordering session active" state machine beyond the two booleans in `community_line_bot_states` — any image from a bound+approved user is treated as an order photo at any time, which was a deliberate simplification per the "不用做複雜的對話管理系統" instruction.

### Production go-live checklist (status unknown — corrected this round, see "Production incident" above)

**This section's original premise ("none of this has been done, this feature only exists on Staging") turned out to be wrong** — a real Production crash this round (`recognizePriceFromPhoto` crashing on every image message in Production logs) proved the LINE webhook is already pointed at Production and this feature's code is already live there, contradicting every prior round's assumption here. Whether the 4 items below were actually done, done partially, or done some other way than described isn't known — this checklist was written on a false premise and was never updated against reality. Don't trust it; confirm current Production state directly (`vercel env ls`, the LINE console's webhook URL, the Production Supabase project's migration history) before assuming anything about Production from this file. Leaving the original items below as a reference for *what to check*, not as a record of what's true:

1. **Apply all 7 migrations to Production separately, in order** (caveat: per the "Production incident" section above, it's no longer safe to assume none of these have already been applied to Production some other way — confirm actual Production migration history before assuming this checklist item is outstanding). Production has its own Supabase project/database — applying a migration on Staging's SQL Editor does **not** touch Production. All must be run manually in the **Production** Supabase SQL Editor:
   - `supabase/migrations/202609290001_community_livestream_orders.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202609290002_community_livestream_photos_storage.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202609300001_community_livestream_purchase_status_two_state.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202609300002_community_livestream_remittances.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202609300003_community_livestream_orders_delete_grant.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202609300004_community_livestream_orders_quantity_lock.sql`
   - `supabase/migrations/202610010001_community_livestream_price_dispute.sql`
2. **Set env vars on Vercel's Production environment specifically**, not just Preview/Staging:
   - `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` — confirmed not set anywhere yet (checked via `vercel env ls`); must be added to Production.
   - `LINE_CHANNEL_SECRET` — confirmed already set on **Preview**; explicitly re-check (`vercel env ls`) that it is also set on **Production** before relying on it there — a Preview-only env var does not carry over.
3. **Only switch the LINE Developers console's webhook URL from Staging to Production once testing is done** — `https://pinkkkuin-staging.vercel.app/api/line/webhook` during testing, then `https://pinkkkuin.vercel.app/api/line/webhook` (or whatever the real Production domain is) for go-live. The console holds exactly one webhook URL for the channel at a time, so it's always pointed at either Staging or Production, never both — flipping it is the actual go-live moment for this feature.
4. **Staging data does not migrate to Production.** Every order/photo created while the webhook points at Staging (all manual testing, including the Rich Menu test-user flow above) lives only in the Staging database/bucket. When the webhook is switched to Production, Production starts completely empty for this feature — no orders, no photos carry over automatically. (This feature no longer creates any `community_line_bindings` rows at all — see 功能一's binding/review removal above.)

## Community LINE notifications (v1 — manual admin batch send)

- **Not automatic.** An earlier version of this round fired notifications automatically on status change (bought/arrived/marketplace-link-set); that was explicitly reverted. The 3 routes it touched (`order-items/[id]` PATCH, `notebooks` PATCH, `shipment-requests/[id]` PATCH) are back to exactly their pre-notification behavior.
- General batch flow (訂單明細): admin checks order rows (same checkboxes the batch status-update feature uses), picks a type from "LINE 通知類型" — now only **通知付款 / 到貨提醒** (賣貨便可下單 was removed from here, see below) — clicks "LINE 通知". Only the customers behind the checked rows are notified.
- `POST /api/backend/community/line-notifications` — body `{ orderIds, kind }`, `kind` validated against `COMMUNITY_LINE_BATCH_NOTIFICATION_KINDS = ['bought','arrived']` — looks up each order's nickname, dispatches via `src/lib/line/community-notifications.ts::sendCommunityOrderNotifications`, returns per-order results + `successCount`/`failedCount` for the toast.
- **Deduped by recipient**: `sendCommunityOrderNotifications` resolves every selected order to its bound LINE user id first, groups by that id, and sends exactly one LINE message per distinct recipient — a buyer with multiple selected orders gets one message, not one per order. Each order in the group still gets its own `community_line_notifications` row and its own entry in the returned results (same outcome as the one send), so status is still visible per selected order.
- **賣貨便可下單 moved to a dedicated per-出貨申請 action**: each card in the 出貨申請 list gets a "發送賣貨便通知" button, shown whenever that card has a `marketplace_url` and isn't `completed`/`cancelled` — **including after it has already been sent once**; the button is never hidden due to prior send history (only the shipment status itself, same as before). The admin never types a link in here. Click → `POST /api/backend/community/shipment-requests/[id]/notify` (empty body) → `sendCommunityShipmentMarketplaceNotification(shipmentRequestId)` reads nickname/order_ids/marketplace_url **fresh from the DB at send time** (so it always reflects whatever was last saved via the 賣貨便連結 input, single or batch, never a stale client value), builds `{{商品清單}}` from its own `order_ids`, sends, and records with `target_id` = the **shipment request id** (not an order id, since this trigger is scoped to one shipment request which may cover several orders for the same customer). The old "送出 LINE 下單通知" button (a manual completion marker that predated real LINE sending — just a confirm() + status→`completed`) has been **removed**; the status dropdown on each card already covers marking a request completed.
- **發送時間 field (no migration)**: each 出貨申請 card shows an independent "發送時間" field with the most recent 賣貨便可下單 send time for that request, or "尚未發送". Sourced from the existing `community_line_notifications` table — `recordNotification` already `upsert`s on `(kind, target_id)` on every send, so `sent_at` is already "most recent send" with zero schema change; `GET /api/backend/community/shipment-requests` now also reads `sent_at` for `kind='marketplace_ready'` per request id (query wrapped in its own try/catch, so a not-yet-migrated table just shows no time instead of breaking the list). After a send, the frontend reloads both the notification log and the shipment list so this field updates immediately.
  - **Known limitation, not built**: because the table is `unique(kind, target_id)`, each resend overwrites the same row — there is **no full per-send history** (only the latest time is ever retrievable). A fully traceable log of every individual send would need a new append-only table (a migration), which was intentionally **not** created this round per instructions to report before any schema change. Flagged here for a decision next round if the full history is actually needed.
- **賣貨便連結 editing — single and batch, no popup**: the 出貨申請 card's 賣貨便連結 field shows a text input pre-filled with the current `marketplaceUrl` + a 保存 button, for any card not `completed`/`cancelled` (those keep the old read-only "開啟連結" display). Single-row 保存 client-validates HTTPS, then calls `updateCommunityShipmentStatus` (same PATCH endpoint, same pending→accepted auto-transition as before). A "一鍵編輯連結" button (top-right of the 出貨申請 section, enabled once ≥1 card is checked) opens a modal that groups the checked requests **by buyer (nickname)** — one buyer with several requests/notebooks/products gets exactly one link input. **Compressed this round** to two lines per buyer: line 1 is `客人名稱 / LINE 暱稱 / 記事本名稱` only, line 2 is just the link input — the previous 商品明細 list and "包含記事本：" line were removed so many more buyers fit on screen without scrolling; `communityShipmentBuyerGroups()` still collects `items`/notebook names internally (harmless, just unused by this modal's markup now). One 保存 fires all the underlying PATCH calls together (`Promise.all`, admin doesn't wait row by row), then the card list reloads so every affected card's inline input shows the new value immediately. Batch save reuses the same single-request PATCH endpoint and pending→accepted transition — no new API, no migration. `editCommunityShipmentMarketplace`/`window.prompt()` remain gone; there is no prompt/popup/new-window anywhere in `lily-backend.html`.
- Recipient resolution (both paths): `community_line_bindings` where `review_status = 'approved'`, matched by nickname.
- Send: `sendLineUserText` in `src/lib/line/client.ts` (same pattern as the existing `sendLineAdminText`/`sendLineUserFlex`), using `LINE_CHANNEL_ACCESS_TOKEN` (confirmed configured on Vercel Preview).
- Result tracking: every attempt (success or failure — no binding, no link, push disabled, provider error) upserts into `community_line_notifications` on `(kind, target_id)`. Admin sees it in the 社群訂單 tab's "LINE 通知紀錄" panel (type / nickname / status pill / error / time), backed by `GET /api/backend/community/line-notifications` (unchanged). Pill wording: `未通知` / **`已送出`** (LINE API 發送請求成功 — tooltip makes clear this is not a read receipt, no "已讀" wording anywhere) / `失敗` (tooltip points at the 失敗原因 column).
- Explicitly out of scope for v1 (per instructions): top-up, payment rejection, and meetup notifications.

### Editable message templates (no migration)

- `src/lib/line/community-notification-templates.ts` holds the 3 default templates verbatim as given, and reads/writes the admin-edited versions through `schedule_settings` (type=`community-line-notification-templates`, JSON in the `image` column) — the same generic-KV pattern `member-line-notifications` already uses. No new table.
- Both send paths fetch the current saved template and render it: `{{商品清單}}` becomes one `系列名稱｜商品名稱 ×數量` line per bought item (bought+arrived items for `kind='arrived'`) across the relevant orders (the recipient's group for 通知付款/到貨提醒; that shipment request's own `order_ids` for 賣貨便可下單), `{{社群訂單連結}}` becomes the fixed `https://pinkkkuin-community-orders.vercel.app`, `{{賣貨便連結}}` becomes that shipment request's own `marketplace_url` (賣貨便可下單 only — never user-typed). Whatever the admin last saved is what goes out — "以當下儲存的模板為準".
- Admin UI: moved off the page body into a green "LINE 通知文案設定" button in the 社群訂單 section's top-right toolbar (matches the LINE brand color, next to the other section-level actions). Click opens a modal listing the 3 types, each with 編輯訊息 opening the same per-type editor modal as before — textarea, 3 insert-variable buttons, 恢復預設文案 (fills the textarea from the default, not saved until 儲存), 儲存 → `POST /api/backend/community/line-notification-templates`. Only the entry point moved; the list markup (`#community-line-template-list`), the per-type editor, and the API are unchanged.
- Batch send, LINE-user-id dedupe, and `community_line_notifications` logging are untouched by this round — only the message-content step changed.

### Blocked: migration not yet applied

- `supabase/migrations/202609230002_community_line_notifications.sql` creates `community_line_notifications` (kind, target_id, nickname, line_user_id, status, error_message, sent_at; unique on `(kind, target_id)`; `service_role` gets select/insert/update only, no delete). This has **not** been run against any database yet — needs to be applied manually in the Staging SQL Editor. No further migration was needed for the manual-send rework — same table, `target_id` just always means "order id" now at the application level.
- Until it's applied, clicking "LINE 通知" still won't error (the write to the log table is caught and logged like any other notification failure), but the 通知紀錄 panel will stay empty and re-clicking won't dedupe via the unique constraint.
- Not yet end-to-end tested (migration pending, and no Staging nickname with an approved LINE binding was available this round). Once the migration is applied: pick a customer with an approved LINE binding, (a) check their order row(s) in 訂單明細 (try selecting 2+ orders for the same customer to confirm the dedupe — only one LINE message should arrive) and send 通知付款/到貨提醒, (b) find/create a 出貨申請 for them with a marketplace_url set and click 發送賣貨便通知 — confirm the button doesn't appear at all when there's no link yet. For both: confirm their LINE received it (check the rendered {{商品清單}}/{{社群訂單連結}}/{{賣貨便連結}} look right) and the 通知紀錄 panel shows 已送出. Also try 編輯訊息 → change a template → send again → confirm the edited text (not the default) is what arrives.

### Blocked: `community_notebooks` metadata migration not yet applied

- `supabase/migrations/202609240001_community_notebooks_metadata.sql` creates `community_notebooks` (notebook_name unique, ordered_date, expected_shipment_month text, expected_shipment_precision enum, expected_shipment_date; service_role select/insert/update only). Backward-compatible — purely additive, doesn't touch `community_orders` or any existing table/RPC. **Not yet run against any database** — needs to be applied manually in the Staging SQL Editor (same as the other pending migration above).
- Until applied: `GET /api/backend/community/notebooks` still works (the meta query is in its own try/catch), 記事本管理 rows just show 訂購日期="未設定"/預計出貨="未定" for every notebook, and saving via the 編輯 modal (`PUT`) will fail with a 500 until the table exists.
- Not yet end-to-end tested for the same reason. Once applied: open 記事本管理, click 編輯 on a notebook, set 訂購日期 + try each 預計出貨 precision (especially 日期確定 vs. the others) and confirm the row's displayed text matches the format examples in AI_HANDOFF (`2026 年 10 月中旬` / `2026 年 11 月底` / `2026/10/18` / `未定`), then confirm 下單月份/預計出貨月份 filters actually narrow the list.

## Current community admin UI

- Shipment request customer cells show only the LINE display name and bound community nickname.
- The main community order table is titled `訂單明細`; duplicate `搶購狀態` and `商品到貨` columns are removed.
- Notes remain text inputs and visually align with adjacent status controls.
- **Notebook management is now a compact table, one row per notebook** (was one `<details>` accordion box per notebook — replaced this round so many notebooks fit on screen at once): each row shows 記事本名稱/訂購日期/預計出貨 plus an 編輯 button and an expand toggle; clicking expand reveals the same order/item detail table as before (LINE name, nickname, product, variant, quantity, unit price, total, purchase status, arrival status — unchanged) in a detail `<tr>` below. Notebook management and the order table still edit the same purchase/arrival data (both read the single `communityOrders` array and both re-render on every `loadCommunityOrders`); which rows are expanded is preserved across re-renders (tracked via a `data-row-id` slug of the notebook name + `.community-notebook-detail-row.open`, same defensive pattern as before, just adapted to the new table markup).
- **訂購日期／預計出貨 metadata (migration, see below)**: 編輯 opens `communityNotebookMetaModal` to set 訂購日期 (plain date) and 預計出貨 as a fuzzy time — a `YYYY-MM` month + a precision (上旬/中旬/下旬/月底/日期確定/未定), with a real date field that only applies when precision is 日期確定. Saved via `PUT /api/backend/community/notebooks` (upsert by notebook name) into a new `community_notebooks` table; `GET` on the same route now also returns these fields per notebook (merged in server-side, wrapped in its own try/catch so a not-yet-migrated table just shows "未設定"/"未定" instead of breaking the list). Display formatting (`formatCommunityNotebookExpectedShipment`): `2026 年 10 月中旬`, `2026 年 11 月底`, `2026/10/18` (exact date), `未定` (no data or precision=unknown). A notebook filter row above the table (name keyword, 下單月份, 預計出貨月份) filters this same client-side list — purely client-side over the already-loaded notebook list, no new query params, doesn't touch 訂單明細's own pagination/filtering.
- Editable status controls render as black-text pills. Purchase, payment, and arrival colors follow the current approved mapping.
- Orders are grouped by the notebook's first source occurrence and keep the existing import/source row order using existing timestamps and IDs.
- **社群訂單 page layout — dividers, not cards (this round)**: on this page only (`#section-community-orders > .backend-member-filterbar`), the pink card look (border/rounded corners/background/box-shadow) was removed and replaced with a plain bottom divider line between blocks (`border-bottom:1px solid #fbcfe8`, no border on the last block) — every other admin page keeps the original card look, this override is scoped to this page's top-level section wrappers only. Section titles were also enlarged (`font-size:0.9rem` → `1.05rem`, still the same weight/blue color) across all 7: 待處理摘要 (no title), 記事本管理, 訂單明細 (merged with the search/filter bar into one block, see next bullet), 付款審核, 出貨申請, LINE 通知紀錄, 匯入紀錄. **Block order** (top to bottom): 待處理摘要 → 記事本管理 → 訂單明細 → 付款審核 → 出貨申請 → LINE 通知紀錄 → 匯入紀錄. (LINE 通知紀錄 was moved in an earlier round from right after 待處理摘要 down to directly above 匯入紀錄 — order only, content/API/behavior untouched.)
- 訂單明細 card: search row (搜尋 input + 匯款狀態/到貨狀態/下單狀態 selects + 搜尋/清除 buttons) is the same card as the order table (previously two separate cards); those 4 fields' gray `<label>` text was removed (inputs/selects keep their function via `aria-label`, so still accessible) — only the search row's labels were touched, everything else keeps its label. 批次修改欄位/修改為/套用到已選項目 sits at the right end of that same search row (previously its own row below in a separate card) — **right-aligned this round** via `margin-left:auto` on the `已選 N 筆` span that starts the group (search/filter fields before it stay left-packed, everything from that span onward gets pushed to the row's right edge). The LINE 通知類型/LINE 通知 button pair sits in the row directly below the search+batch row; its own gray label was removed, the button is LINE green (`.btn-line`), and **this round** the whole row got `justify-content:flex-end` so the pair sits at the right edge of its own row too.
- **出貨申請 is now a standard table** (this round — was a card list with a custom expand toggle): header/row-height/spacing/font-size/alignment now match 記事本管理/LINE 通知紀錄 exactly (same `.backend-member-table` class). Columns: checkbox, 客人 / LINE 暱稱 (`暱稱 / LINE 顯示名稱`), 申請時間, 包含記事本, 目前狀態 dropdown, 賣貨便連結 (inline input+保存, or read-only link once completed/cancelled), 發送時間 (see LINE notifications section above), 操作 (發送賣貨便通知 when a link is saved), and a 商品明細 expand toggle. 商品明細 still expands per-notebook `商品名稱 ×數量` (unchanged content, sourced from `notebookGroups`) but now as a table detail `<tr>` (same technique as 記事本管理's detail row) instead of a div — collapsed rows add zero extra height to the main table. Which rows are expanded is preserved across re-renders (`data-request-id` + `.community-shipment-detail-row.open`). Nothing about the underlying PATCH/notify/save logic changed — only the markup. A "一鍵編輯連結" button (top-right, enabled once ≥1 row is checked) still opens the batch link-editing modal described above (that modal itself still uses `.community-shipment-card` styling — unaffected by this table conversion, it's a separate popup). **This round**: the expanded 商品明細 content (記事本名稱 heading + 商品名稱 ×數量 rows) is left-aligned, not centered — added `#section-community-orders .backend-member-table .community-shipment-detail-row td { text-align: left; }`, which needs that extra specificity to beat the page's blanket `#section-community-orders .backend-member-table td { text-align: center; }` rule; only this detail row is affected, every other column in the main table stays centered.
- LINE 文案設定 modal's per-type "編輯訊息" button got `white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0` so its text never wraps regardless of the row's remaining width (the long-preview text on the left shrinks instead, since it already has `min-width:0`).
- **LINE 通知紀錄 (this round)**: the inner `.page-header` wrapping its title + 重新整理 button got `border-bottom:none;padding-bottom:0` — removes the divider line that used to sit between the title row and the table below it. The block-level divider between this whole card and the next one (匯入紀錄), from the page's `#section-community-orders > .backend-member-filterbar` border-bottom, is untouched.
- **付款審核 table field cleanup (this round, display only)**: 送出時間/處理時間 now show date on line 1, time on line 2 (new `communityDateTimeStacked` helper, used only for these two columns — the shared `storefrontDateTime` used everywhere else on the page is untouched). 記事本明細 column renamed to 記事本名稱 and now shows just the notebook name(s), one per line — the gray 商品/折抵/基準 sub-line under each notebook was removed. Column renames: 商品總額→總金額, 記事本折抵→預留, 應付總額→匯款金額. Columns removed from display: 累計已收, 尚需補款, 多匯金額 — their underlying values (`cumulativeReceived`/`remainingAmount`/`overpaidAmount`) are untouched in the API/data and `communityPaymentReviewActions`'s 核准/需補款/多匯待退款 label logic still reads them internally, they're just no longer rendered as columns.
- **社群名單 panel order (new this round)**: 待處理名單 is now the top panel, 已綁定名單 below it (was the other way around) — a pure HTML reorder of the two `<section class="community-member-panel">` blocks inside `.community-member-grid`, nothing else touched. The two tbody ids (`community-approved-member-list`/`community-pending-member-list`) and every bit of load/render/search/bulk-approve JS keyed off them are untouched — none of that logic is order-dependent, it all targets elements by id regardless of where they sit in the DOM.

## Backend-wide search/filter UI unification (this round)

Every real search/filter bar across the whole admin (not just community-orders) was brought to one visual spec: no external gray `<label>` above a field, the field's purpose shown directly in it, and identical control height/radius/padding/border everywhere. Purely display — no filter value, filter logic, API, or DB changed.

- **Labels removed, replaced by**: `input`/`select` get `aria-label` (accessible name preserved); `input` placeholders are the field's own purpose text (e.g. "商品名稱", "社群暱稱、記事本名稱"); `select` default/`value=""` options were renamed from bare "全部" to self-descriptive text — "全部匯款狀態", "全部到貨狀態", "全部下單狀態", "全部 LINE 狀態", "全部會員狀態", "全部訂單類型/訂單狀態/付款狀態/配送狀態", "全部出貨狀態", "全部廠商", "全部購物網站", "全部分類", "全部收支類型", "全部集運公司" — so a row of unlabeled selects still reads correctly at a glance. Two of these (`filter-v-vendor`/`filter-w-vendor`) are populated dynamically by `populateFilterVendors()`, so the default-option text was changed in that JS string, not just the static HTML.
- **Date/month fields (no native placeholder support)**: `<input type="date">`/`type="month">` ignore the `placeholder` attribute in every browser, so a light-gray overlay hint (`.admin-filter-hint`, absolutely positioned inside a `position:relative` wrapper) shows "開始日期"/"結束日期"/"下單月份"/"預計出貨月份" etc. and disappears once a value is picked. Mechanism: `syncAdminDateHint()` toggles an `.admin-hint-empty` class on the input (which sets `color:transparent` so the input's own native empty-state text doesn't show underneath the hint) on `input`/`change`, plus a one-time init pass on page load — but **only for inputs that actually have an `.admin-filter-hint` sibling**. Every other date input in the admin (add/edit modals, `community-notebook-meta-*`, `ledger-date` in the add-entry modal, meetup/schedule create forms, etc.) is completely unaffected — this was deliberately scoped after catching that a naive "all date inputs" version would have made every modal date field look blank when empty.
- **Unified control style**: `.backend-member-filter-field`/`.storefront-order-filter-field` were already identical (`min-height:34px; border:1px solid #fbcfe8; border-radius:0.72rem; padding:0.42rem 0.62rem; color:#334155; font-size:0.82rem; font-weight:800`, focus `border-color:#f472b6; box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(244,114,182,.12)`) — that became the baseline. Three previously-divergent systems were brought up to the same values: plain `.filter-bar .form-group` (廠商叫貨明細/購物網站叫貨明細/客戶訂單明細), `.product-filter-card .form-group` (現貨/預購商品管理), and a new `.schedule-filter-card` marker class added to the 擺攤時程/連線時程 search rows (they had no distinguishing class before, needed one to scope the CSS safely without touching the many other unrelated `.form-row`/`.table-wrapper` uses elsewhere in modals). 總帳表's `.ledger-filter-card` has its own local `<style>` block inside that section — edited in place to the same values. `.tag-select-button` (the IP/商品類別 multi-select trigger) got the same border/radius/height too. A global `input::placeholder { color:#94a3b8; }` was added (harmless everywhere, including modals — pure color, no behavior change).
- **Full page/field scope**: 會員管理 (搜尋/LINE 狀態/會員狀態), 社群訂單頁's 記事本管理/訂單明細/出貨申請 (搜尋 fields already de-labeled in an earlier round — this round just fixed their selects' bare "全部" text and did the notebook filters), 社群名單 (LINE 名稱/申請暱稱 — already had no label, just added `aria-label`), 訂單管理 (搜尋 + 開始/結束日期 + 4 selects), 客戶訂單明細, 廠商叫貨明細, 購物網站叫貨明細, 現貨商品管理, 預購商品管理, 擺攤時程, 連線時程, 總帳表, 集運帳表 (`#freight-company-filter` — found to be a static mockup with hardcoded table rows and no wired-up filter logic at all; changed anyway for visual consistency, zero functional risk since nothing reads it).
- **Explicitly NOT touched** (not search/filter UI, so left exactly as-is): 批次修改欄位/修改為 (batch-edit config, community-orders), LINE 通知類型 selector, 面交時段/擺攤/連線 schedule *create* forms (date/time/location fields for adding a new slot, as opposed to the search row above the table), all add/edit modals (vendor, website, product, ledger entry, brand/appearance settings, community member/order edit, etc.), and all page-size/pagination selects (`#ledger-page-size`, `#community-order-page-size`, etc. — a different UI purpose from search/filter, and most already show self-descriptive option text like "20 筆" without a label).

## Backend-wide mobile UX fixes (across four rounds)

Found while manually testing the new 社群連線訂單 page on a phone. The overlap fixes below are two **shared, global** backend components, not that page's own `.community-livestream-*` styles (which were already confirmed fine and untouched). The nav-collapse and topbar/search-row items are deliberate exceptions scoped to mobile screens only, added in later rounds on top of that. All of this is a small prep step for the larger future "後台 app 化" (turning the admin into a proper mobile app) initiative, not a fix specific to any one feature. CSS/inline-style only — no API, no migration; one JS-logic question was investigated and deliberately left alone (see below).

- **Mobile nav collapsed to community-only**: on screens ≤720px, the sidebar now only shows the `社群管理` menu group (社群訂單/社群名單/面交管理/社群連線訂單) — every other module (會員管理, 訂單管理, 叫貨管理, 商品管理, 擺攤連線管理, 帳表管理, etc.) is hidden from the nav entirely. One rule: `.sidebar-menu > .menu-group:not([data-module-key="community"]) { display: none; }`, inside the same existing `@media (max-width: 720px)` block. Purely a nav-visibility change — no data/API/permission change, and a hidden module's own page is still reachable by whatever other means might open it (e.g. a tab restored from a prior desktop session); this was explicitly not required to be blocked. Desktop (outside this media query) is completely unaffected — every module still shows. There is currently no "switch back to full nav" escape hatch on mobile, per instructions.

- **`.page-header`/`.toolbar` didn't wrap on narrow screens** — these two classes back the title+action-button row at the top of every single backend page/section (`lily-backend.html`, originally defined with no `flex-wrap` and no mobile override at all). On a phone this squeezed the title and every toolbar button into one unwrapped row. Fixed inside the existing `@media (max-width: 720px)` block (same breakpoint every other mobile override in this file already uses): `.page-header { flex-direction: column; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.75rem; }` (title stacks above the toolbar) and `.toolbar { flex-wrap: wrap; }` (buttons wrap instead of overflowing). Desktop is untouched — both rules only exist inside this mobile media query.
- **`.environment-badge` (the fixed orange STAGING pill) overlapped the topbar on mobile — actual cause was `.topbar-right`, not `.toolbar`.** Investigated further than the original bug report assumed: the "調整模塊順序" button that visually collided with the badge lives in `.topbar-right` (inside the shared `<header class="topbar">`, confirmed via `grep` — there is no `.toolbar` anywhere inside `.topbar`), a completely different component from the per-page `.page-header`/`.toolbar`. At the time, `.topbar`/`.topbar-right`/`.sidebar` had **no mobile handling at all** in this codebase (no wrap, no collapse, no hide-on-narrow) — `.sidebar` gained its own dedicated open/close/width mobile behavior in a later round (see below); `.topbar`/`.topbar-right` still have none, that remains real scope for the future app-化 project. As a minimal, same-file, same-breakpoint fix that doesn't touch `.topbar`/`.sidebar`: `.environment-badge { top: 64px; right: 0.75rem; }` on mobile only — moves the fixed badge to just below the 60px-tall topbar, clearing both the topbar's own right-side buttons and the now-stacked `.page-header` (whose top-right corner is empty on mobile since the toolbar moved below the title).
- **Floating 修改密碼/登出 buttons (`src/app/backend/route.ts`, `wrap.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;right:16px;bottom:16px;...'`) covered the bottom of scrolled content on mobile.** Left that component's positioning logic completely untouched (not moved, not resized) — instead gave the scrollable content area clearance: `.content { padding: 1rem 1rem 80px; }` inside the same existing `@media (max-width: 720px)` block (was `padding: 1rem;`), so a user can scroll the last card/field on any page fully clear of the floating buttons.
- **topbar further simplified on mobile (this round)**: the hamburger (`#menuToggle`) stays (still needed to switch between the community tabs on mobile), but three more things now hide on ≤720px screens — the plain-text "回到主選單" link (`.topbar-home-link`), the "調整模塊順序" button (`.topbar-module-order-btn`), and the whole open-tabs strip (`#tabsContainer`, since mobile only ever shows one page at a time and `.page-title` already says which). All three: display-only `display:none` inside the existing mobile media query, DOM/JS untouched, desktop unaffected.
- **`openTab()`/`openTabs` investigated, deliberately left alone**: was asked to check whether hiding `#tabsContainer` on mobile could leave stale "open tab" state behind. Traced `openTab()`/`activateTab()`/`closeTab()` (all shared with desktop, no viewport branching today) — `openTabs` is a plain array that only grows (a tab is only removed by explicitly closing it, which mobile has no UI for now that the strip is hidden), but `activateTab()` unconditionally hides every `.section-container` and shows only the target one on every navigation, so **the visible content is always correct regardless of how large `openTabs` gets** — this is unbounded array growth over a session, not a visible or functional bug. Changing `openTab()` to "replace instead of append" on mobile would need a viewport check inside this shared function (risk of subtly affecting desktop's own multi-tab behavior if done carelessly), so per instructions this was **not** changed — flagged here as a known, low-priority, non-visible characteristic rather than fixed.
- **Search-row fields narrower + icon-only buttons on mobile**: pages sharing `.backend-member-filter-row`/`.backend-member-filter-field` (會員管理, 記事本管理, 訂單明細, 出貨申請, 社群連線訂單) get, on ≤720px only: `.backend-member-filter-field { min-width: 0 !important; flex: 1 1 120px; }` (the `!important` is needed because several of these fields set their own `min-width:220px`/`260px` inline, which a plain media-query rule can't otherwise override) so the search box no longer forces the row's 搜尋/清除 buttons onto their own line; and those buttons' label text (搜尋/清除, 5 button-pairs total) is now wrapped in `<span class="btn-label">`, hidden via `.backend-member-filter-row .btn-label { display:none; }` on mobile — icon stays, text stays in the DOM (so screen readers still get it), just visually hidden rather than removed. Other buttons in the same rows (批次修改/套用到已選項目/etc.) were **not** touched — only the ones explicitly wrapped in `.btn-label` are affected by that selector, nothing wrapped means nothing else is hidden.
- **Sidebar: default-closed, click-outside-to-close, width auto-fits content on mobile (new this round)**: previously `.sidebar` inherited the desktop `width: 260px` default even on mobile (open-by-default), `#menuToggle`'s click handler was the only way to open/close it (`sb.style.width` toggled between the literal strings `'0px'`/`'260px'`), and there was no click-outside-to-close at all. Now, on ≤720px only: `.sidebar { width: 0; max-width: 80vw; }` sets the CSS default to closed (the `max-width` is a defensive cap for `max-content` sizing below, not expected to ever bind in practice given how short the Chinese module labels are); the open/close logic was refactored into two small named functions, `openSidebar()`/`closeSidebar()`, both called by `#menuToggle`'s click handler exactly as before **and** by a new `document`-level click listener that closes the sidebar whenever the click target is neither inside `#sidebar` nor `#menuToggle` itself (excluding `#menuToggle` specifically is what stops the very click that *opens* the sidebar from being immediately treated as an "outside click" and closing it again) — gated behind `isMobileSidebarLayout()` (a `window.matchMedia('(max-width: 720px)')` check) so this listener is a no-op on desktop. `openSidebar()` now also picks the open width conditionally: `max-content` on mobile (auto-fits to whatever the longest visible module label needs, replacing the fixed 260px) vs. the unchanged `260px` on desktop. One subtlety that needed its own fix: a CSS default of `width: 0` alone isn't enough for `#menuToggle`'s existing `sb.style.width === '0px'` string-comparison to work correctly on the very first click (the inline `style.width` property reads as an empty string until JS actually sets it, not `'0px'`, so the first tap would silently no-op) — so page load now calls `closeSidebar()` once up front when `isMobileSidebarLayout()` is true, which primes the inline style to the literal `'0px'` the toggle logic expects. Desktop is completely unaffected by any of this — `isMobileSidebarLayout()` false means the original 260px-fixed, no-outside-click behavior continues unchanged.
- **Verification done**: `npm run build` PASS every round; inline `<script>` syntax check (`new Function()` over every block) — `ALL OK` every round; redeployed and re-aliased to `pinkkkuin-staging.vercel.app` after each round, confirmed both `/backend` (login gate) and the community route still return `200`.
- **Not verified**: an actual rendered mobile screenshot at ~375px, for any round's changes (header/floating-button overlap, nav collapse, topbar/search-row simplification, or this round's sidebar open/close/width behavior). This environment has no browser/screenshot tool (checked — no Playwright/Puppeteer installed, no browser automation tool available), and the admin page itself is login-gated so an unauthenticated `curl` only ever sees the login form, never the real dashboard HTML. The fixes above are based on precise reading of the actual CSS/DOM structure (confirmed via `grep`/`Read`, not guessed) and manual reasoning about the `style.width` string comparisons and event-bubbling order (button click fires and updates `sb.style.width` before the same click event bubbles up to the new `document` listener), not a rendered screenshot. **Please do a real on-device check** (社群連線訂單 title vs. STAGING badge, list-bottom note field vs. the floating account buttons, the nav showing only 社群管理 on mobile, topbar showing only the hamburger, search rows fitting search/clear icons on one line without the field forcing a wrap, the sidebar starting closed on page load, opening to a width that fits the longest label without wrapping, tapping a menu item working normally, and tapping outside the sidebar closing it, and at least one other existing page at the same width) before considering this closed.

## Backend PWA — Phase 1 (installable only, no offline caching)

First step of a multi-phase plan to make the backend feel more like a standalone app on mobile ("加到手機主畫面" — no browser chrome, its own home-screen icon). This phase is **install-capability only** — no layout changes (a bigger visual redesign is a separate future phase, explicitly not done here) and no offline support (deliberately — this is a management tool, every screen must show live data, so the service worker does nothing but exist). Scoped entirely to `/backend`; nothing under the storefront/member/shop was touched.

- **New static files (main repo, not the submodule)**: `public/backend/manifest.json` (`name`/`short_name` = "Pinkkkuin 後台管理"/"Pinkkkuin 後台", `start_url` and `scope` both `/backend` — so the installed app's navigation never escapes the backend, `display: "standalone"` — no address bar/browser chrome when launched from the home screen, `theme_color`/`background_color` matching the existing brand pink `#ec4899`/`#fdf2f8`), `public/backend/sw.js` (**only** `install`/`activate` listeners, `skipWaiting()`/`clients.claim()` so a redeploy's new SW takes over promptly — no `fetch` listener, no Cache Storage use anywhere, by design), and `public/backend/icons/` (`icon-192.png`, `icon-512.png`, `apple-touch-icon-180.png` — solid `#ec4899` background with a plain white "P", generated with Python/Pillow since no other image tool was available in this environment; these are explicitly **placeholder** art, filenames/location chosen so swapping in real logo files later is a drop-in replacement, not a code change).
